char *timeconv(d)
unsigned d[]; /* binary date array [2] */
{
 unsigned hour;
 static char timestr[12];
 strcpy(timestr,"           "); /* zero out old time. */
 hour = d[1] / 3600;
 tsprintf(timestr, "%2u:%02u:%02u %s", hour == 0 ? 12 : hour,
  (d[1]%3600)/60, d[1]%60, d[0] & 01 ? "pm" : "am");
 return(timestr);
}
char *date()
{
 unsigned bindate[2];
 get_date(bindate);
 return(dateconv(bindate));
}
char *time()
{
 unsigned bindate[2];
 get_date(bindate);
 return(timeconv(bindate));
}
unsigned secdiff(bindate1, bindate2)
/* Procedure secdiff - return difference in seconds bindate2 - bindate1.
If the difference is more than 18.2 hours, then 65535 is returned. */
unsigned bindate1[], bindate2[];
{
 unsigned datediff, secs, secdiff;
 secs = bindate2[1] - bindate1[1];
 if (bindate1[0] == bindate2[0])
  return(secs);
 else {
  datediff = count_days(bindate2) - count_days(bindate1);
        datediff = datediff * 2 + (bindate2[0] & 0x1) - (bindate1[0] & 0x1);
  secdiff = bindate2[1] - bindate1[1];
  secs = 43200 + secdiff;
/* datediff now has the difference in dates in twelve hour intervals. */
  if (datediff > 2 || datediff == 1 && secdiff >= 22336
   || datediff == 2 && secs >= 22336)
        return(65535);
  else /* datediff must be 1 or 2. */
   return(secs + 43200 * (datediff - 1));
  }
}
unsigned mindiff(bindate1, bindate2)
/* Procedure mindiff - return the difference in minutes bindate2 - bindate1.
The difference is truncated; any remaining seconds are discarded.
If the difference is more than 22 days, then 22 days (31680 min) is
returned. */
unsigned bindate1[], bindate2[];
{
 unsigned datediff, min, sec1, sec2;
 
 sec1 = bindate1[1];
 sec2 = bindate2[1];
 min = (sec2 - sec1) / 60;
 if (bindate1[0] == bindate2[0])
  return(min);
 else {
  datediff = count_days(bindate2) - count_days(bindate1);
  datediff = datediff * 2 + (bindate2[0] & 0x1) - (bindate1[0] & 0x1);
  min = (43200 + sec2 - sec1) / 60;
  if (datediff > 44) /* 22 days. */
   return(31680);  /* 22 days max. */
  else
   return(min + (datediff - 1) * 720);
 }
}
       
int count_days(bindate)
/* Procedure day_count - count the number of days since Jan 1st, 1980. */
unsigned bindate[];
{
 int  month, day, year;
 day = (bindate[0] >> 1) & 0x3F;
 month = (bindate[0] >> 8) & 0xF;
 year = bindate[0] >> 12;
 return(year_count[year] + day_count[year % 4 == 0][month] + day);
}

} GL_SYSTEM_DATA;

reset_parse()
/* Procedure reset_parse - reset the parse counter. */
{
 parseloc = 0;
}
upper_parse(buffer, word)
/* Procedure parse - extract a single word from 'buffer' to 'word'.
The size of 'word' must equal the size of 'buffer' to avoid over-
flowing the word string.    The procedure returns the length of the
word, zero if end-of-string. Words are not* converted to lowercase. */
char *buffer, *word;
{
int wordpos;  /* character pointer for 'word'.*/
char ch;
 if (parseloc == -1) { /* end of buffer already encountered. */
  word[0] = '\0'; /* guarantee end of string. */
  return(0);
 }
 while ((ch = buffer[parseloc++]) != '\0')
  if (!isseparator(ch)) /* skip separators. */
   break;
 if (ch == '\0') { /* return zero if no non-seps found. */
  parseloc = -1;
        word[0] = '\0'; /* guarantee end of string. */
  return(0);
 }
 wordpos = 0;
 do {  /* fill word */
  word[wordpos++] = ch;
     ch = buffer[parseloc++];
 } while ((ch != '\0') && !isseparator(ch) && wordpos < BUFLEN - 1);
 word[wordpos] = '\0'; /* add trailing zero to make proper string. */
 if (ch == '\0') /* end of buffer? */
  parseloc = -1;
 else {
  while ((ch = buffer[parseloc++]) != '\0')
   if (!isseparator(ch)) /* skip separators. */
    break;
  parseloc--;
  if (ch == '\0')
   parseloc = -1;
 }
 return(wordpos);
}
 
parse(string, word)
/* Parse - parse string into lowercase words.  
This procedure is identical to upper_parse, except it converts words
to lowercase. */
char *string, *word;
{
 int  len;
 len = upper_parse(string, word);
 string_tolower(word);  /* convert to lowercase. */
 if (len <= 3) /* mask out short prepositions and articles. */
  if ( equal(word,"is") ||
    equal(word,"the") ||
    equal(word,"to") ||
    equal(word,"in") ||
    equal(word,"at")
  )
   return(parse(string, word));  /* recursively get the next word. */
 return(len);
}

main()
/* This program prompts for a file name, and then copies that file 
four times to the standard output. */
{
 char  file_name[20];  /* file name. */
 for (;;) { /* loop forever until break-key pressed. */
     
  printf("enter a filename to print? ");
  getstr(file_name, 20); /* read in file name. */
  printf("Copying file %s four times:\n",file_name);
  copy_four_times(file_name); /* display file four times. */  
 }
} /* end of main program */

/* This procedure copies the file "name" four times.  It rewinds the file
between each copy.  (The rewind is where the bug occurs!) */
{
 char buffer[80];   /* line buffer for output. */
 FILE *fp;
 extern FILE *fopen();
 int  i;            /* loop counter.  loop four times. */
 fp = fopen(name,"r");  /* <-- Bug occurs in read-only mode.  */
 for (i=0; i<4; i++) {  /* print file four times in a row. */
  rewind(fp);  /* <-- bug! file gets randomly switched with other task's
      file pointer.  This happens when both tasks are doing
      file I/O at the same time. */
  while (fgetstr(fp, buffer, 80) >= 0)
   printf("%s\n",buffer);  /* copy file to output.  Notice how the 
            output suddenly changes to another
            active file. */
 }
 fclose(fp);
 return(0);
}

#define IDLE 0x0004
#define MONITOR_PORT 23
struct termlist_type {

/*  monitor - Central System Terminal Monitor.
  83/09/24. A. E. Klietz.
  This program monitors the activity on all dial-up terminals.
it sleeps for MONSLEEP_SECS seconds and wakes up to see if any
terminal tasks have died due to hangup or fatal program error. When
the monitor discovers a terminal whose taskid reverts back to IDLE,
it forces the modem to hang up the phone by zeroing the DTR bit in the
Modem Control Register.  It also resets the terminal's default 
characteristics and sends a message to the accounting administrator
saying the user logged out.
  Another task of the monitor is to check for no carrier.  If
a terminal loses it's carrier, then the monitor issues a hangup_exception
to the task phone by zeroing the DTR bit in the Modem Control Register
for two seconds.
  When the monitor detects a new carrier, it escapes a GamBit shell
to handle the login procedures.
  The monitor also resets the gl_monitor byte for all other ttys
when a tty that has been monitoring other ttys hangs up.
  The monitor also updates the peak/nonpeak flag.
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include <io.h>
#include <dev.h>
#include "aekdef.h"
#include "termdef.h"
#include "ac_msgs.h"
#include "globdef.h"
#include "appdef.h"
#define  EXC_HANGUP  0x0001
#define  RUN_BACKGROUND 0x0001
/* flag for setting debug mode. */
/* #define DEBUG */
extern unsigned  My_tid, Dads_tid;
extern char My_tty, My_priority;
struct gl_user_datum  *user_data_ptr;
struct gl_system_datum  *system_data_ptr;
main(argc, argv) 
int  argc;
char *argv[];
{
 char *version = "1.3";
 int  i, loopcount;
 unsigned taskid; /* id of current running task on device. */
    struct termlist_type *term;
 unsigned msreg; /* modem status register port. */
 FILE *fp;
 sleep(2); /* wait so we dont mix output with foreground task. */
 tprintf("\nCentral Terminal Monitor ver %s.\n",version);
 
 if (argc == 0) {  /* query. */
  tfprintf(stderr,"monitor &  -  initiate terminal monitor.\n");
  exit(0);
 }
 if (My_tid == tty_tid(My_tty))
  abort("fatal:monitor - The monitor must run as a background task.\n");
 if (attach(MONITOR_PORT) != 0)
  abort("fatal:monitor - Another monitor task is already active!\n");
/* KLUDGE  comment out bootup check until we know how to do it right. !!!
 if (Dads_tid != 0x0105) 
  error("fatal:monitor - can be run only from bootup. %04x.\n",Dads_tid);
*/
/* The following is the initialization loop for the monitor. */
 for (i=0; i < TERMLIST_LEN; i++)
  if (termlist[i].enabled) {
   term = &termlist[i];
   term->taskid = tty_tid(term->ttynum);
   if (term->taskid == -1)  /* what? */
    error("fatal:monitor - nonexistant tty: %d.\n",term->ttynum);    
   if (term->taskid == IDLE)  {
    init_terminal(term);
    setup_stty(term);
   }
  }
  set_glob(); /* set global pointers. */
  check_timeout();  /* set up peak/nonpeak flag */
  tprintf("The GamBit Timeshare System is ready for operations on %s, %s.\n",
   date(), time());
/* The following is the main loop for the monitor. */
 while (TRUE) { /* loop forever. */
  for (i = 0; i < TERMLIST_LEN; i++)
   if (termlist[i].enabled) {  /* if disabled then ignore port. */
    term = &termlist[i];
    taskid = tty_tid(term->ttynum);
    if ((taskid == IDLE) && (term->taskid != IDLE)) { /* do hangup processing. */
     term->taskid = IDLE;
     send_accounting_logoff(term);
     clear_monitorflag(term->ttynum);
     setup_stty(term); /* set up login config. */
     tprintf("%s  %s info:monitor - no task for tty%d.\n",
      date(), time(), term->ttynum);
    } /* idle */
    else
     term->taskid = taskid;
    if (term->iobase != 0) /* check carrier change. */
     if ((msreg = io_in(term->iobase+6)) & 0x0008)
      if ((msreg & 0x0080) && (taskid == IDLE)) { /* connected. */
                            if (@&system_data_ptr->gl_sysidle) {
tfprintf((fp = fopen(term->name,"wv")),"idle:monitor - system is down.\n");
        fclose(fp);
       }
       else {
/* Start up the GamBit shell so the user can log in to the system. */
        tprintf("%s  %s info:monitor - connection on tty%d.\n",
         date(), time(), term->ttynum);
         escape_tty(term->ttynum, "/cmds/sh");
       }
      }
      else { /* hangup. */
       /* set hangup and thus kill task. */
       set_exception(taskid, EXC_HANGUP, 0);
       tprintf("%s  %s info:monitor - hangup on tty%d.\n",    
        date(), time(), term->ttynum);
      }
   } /* scan */
   if (loopcount >= 20) { /* time to check for timeouts. */
    loopcount = 0;
    check_timeout();
   }
   loopcount++;
   breakable(); /* allow breaks while sleeping. */
   sleep(MONSLEEP_SECS);  /* go to sleep for a few seconds. */
   unbreakable();  /* no interrupts now! */
 } /* loop forever */
 abort("fatal:monitor - unexpected termination.\n");
}
setup_stty(term)
/* Procedure setup_stty - reset the modem to a login configuration. 
 This procedure will set the attributes of the terminal as follows:
  1) Change the tty_RUBOUT to a backspace (0x0008)
  2) Change the line-delete character to control-u.
  3) Disable input gating.
  4) Set full duplex, XON/XOFF, line editing, and tab expanding.
        5) Disconnect phone for modem devices.
  6) set 300 baud 8 bits, 1 stop bit, no parity.
*/
struct termlist_type *term;
{
 FILE *fp;
 struct dev_entry buffer;   /* attribute block buffer. */
 unsigned mcreg; /* modem control register port */
#ifdef DEBUG
 tprintf("debug:monitor - Device attribute set: %s.\n",term->name);
#endif
 fp = fopen(term->name,"wv");
 if (fp == 0) {  /* unable to access modem. */
  tfprintf(stderr,"warn:monitor - Unable to open tty%d.\n",term->ttynum);
  return;
 }
 get_attr(fp, &buffer);
 if (buffer.tty_type == DEV_ASCII) 
  buffer.tty_RUBOUT = 0x0008; /* change delete to backspace. */
 else
  buffer.tty_RUBOUT = 0x007F; /* delete key. */
 buffer.tty_CANCEL = 0x0015; /* change line-erase to cntl-u. */
 set_attr(fp, &buffer); /* set attributes */
 set_option(fp, get_option(fp) & ~IGATE | ECHO | EDIT | XON_XOFF | ETAB);
 fclose(fp);
 if (term->iobase != 0) {  /* if dial-up port */
  /* force hangup by resetting DTR bit on modem. */
  mcreg = io_in(term->iobase+4);
  io_out(term->iobase+4,mcreg & ~0x0001);
  sleep(2); /* wait for phone to disconnect. */
  io_out(term->iobase+3, 0x83); /* 8 bits, no parity, access baud rate. */
  io_out(term->iobase+0, 0x80); /* lower byte, 300 baud. */
  io_out(term->iobase+1, 0x01); /* upper byte, 300 baud. */
  io_out(term->iobase+3, 0x03); /* deaccess baud rate */
  io_out(term->iobase+4,mcreg);
 }
}
init_terminal(term)                   
/* Procedure init_terminal - initialize port at boot-up.   
This procedure will flush the input and output buffers, and 
set the shell-escape character.  For modems, it also sends
the necessary initialization codes for auto-answering. */
struct termlist_type *term;
{
 FILE *fw, *fr;
 struct dev_entry buffer;
 fw = fopen(term->name, "wv");
 fr = fopen(term->name, "rv");
 if (fr == 0 || fw == 0) { /* unable to open modem. */
  tfprintf(stderr,"warn:monitor - Unable to init tty%d.\n",term->ttynum);
  return(0);
 }

/*  sh - user login shell.
  83/09/25. A. E. Klietz.
  'sh' is the login program that replaces the QNX shell
(/cmds/sh) for doing login dialogue with a user.  If 'sh' is
called from another task, then it will transform itself into 
the standard QNX shell, renamed /cmds/qnxsh.  The program is     
activated by pressing cr on the terminal.  It requires a monitor
program to change the shell-escape character to cr beforehand.  
  When activated, this program issues a login header,              
verifies the user, and then transforms itself into the GamBit 
menu program.  
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include <dev.h>
#include "aekdef.h"
#include "globdef.h"
#include "ac_msgs.h"
/*  #define DEBUG  */
#define BEQUEATH_TTY 0x04
#define RUN_CONCURRENT  0x02
#define IDLE   0x0004
extern unsigned My_tid, Dads_tid;
extern char My_tty, My_priority;
struct gl_user_datum  *user_data_ptr;
struct gl_system_datum  *system_data_ptr;
main(argc, argv) 
int  argc;
char *argv[];
{
 if ((Dads_tid != IDLE) || (My_tty == 0)) { /* called from another shell */
  if (argc == 0) { /* query */
   tfprintf(stdout,"use: sh [+s] [+t] [+v] cmd_name [cmd_args]*.\n");
   exit(0);
  }
#ifdef DEBUG
  print("dad: %04x. Me: %04x. My_tty: %d. tty_tid(tty): %04x.\n",
    Dads_tid, My_tid, My_tty, tty_tid(My_tty));
#endif
   trans_QNXshell(argc, argv);
 }
 else  /* set up terminal, do login, and call menu. */
  login_terminal(argc, argv); 
  
 error("sh:fatal - Unexpected termination.\n");
}
login_terminal(argc,argv)
/* Procedure login_dialogue - issue login messages, and verify user. */
int  argc;
char *argv[];
{
 boolean  validuser = FALSE;
 int   ch, success, trycount = 0;
    unsigned taskid;
    char  username[12];
 struct  user_datum  *user_ptr;
 struct  ac_constmsg  constmsg;
 struct  ac_constmsg_reply constmsg_reply;
 struct  dev_entry  buffer;
 unbreakable();
 set_glob();  /* set global data pointers. */
 constmsg.ac_checkword = AC_CHECKWORD;
 constmsg.ac_msgnum   = CONSTMSG;
 if (send(detach(ACCOUNTING_PORT),&constmsg, &constmsg_reply, 
   sizeof(constmsg)) <= 0)
  error("fatal:sh - accounting admin failure..\n");
 get_attr(stdin, &buffer);
 if (buffer.tty_type == DEV_ASCII) {
  print("Press return:\005 ");
  buffer.tty_options &= ~EDIT & ~MAPCR; /* disable line buffering */
  set_attr(stdin, &buffer);
  while ((ch = getchar()) != 0x0D && ch != 0x8D)
   print("%04x\n",ch)
   ;  
  if (ch != 0x0D) /* set even parity. */
   io_out(buffer.tty_ioport+3, 0x1A);      
  buffer.tty_options |= EDIT | MAPCR; /* enable line buffering */
  set_attr(stdin, &buffer);
 }
 
 while (char_waiting(stdin)) /* ignore chars before 1st prompt. */
  getchar();
    print("\n  _\n");
 print(" GamBit Timeshare %s\n",
  constmsg_reply.ac_sysloc);
 print("%s, %s  Port: tty%u\n",     
  date(), time(), My_tty);
 print("%s  Tid: %04x.  %s\n",
  @&system_data_ptr->gl_peakflag ? "Peak time." : "non-peak time.",
  My_tid, constmsg_reply.ac_sysnam);
 while (!validuser && (trycount < 4)) {  /* get usernumber/password. */
  trycount++;
  success = getunpw(username);
  if (success != 0)
   set_glob(); /* set terminal characteristics */
  switch (success) {
case 0: if (trycount < 4) /* dont print on last try */
   print("Improper login, try again.\n"); 
  break;
case 1: validuser = TRUE; 
  break;
case 2: print("Sorry, but you have run out of credits for this month.\n\n");
  print_renew_msg();
  hangup();
  break;
case 3: error("fatal:sh - Bad validuz read.\n");
  break;
case 4: print("Your account has been closed, sorry.\n");
  print_renew_msg();
  hangup();
  break;
case 5: print("Your account balance is nearly expired.\n");
  print_renew_msg();
  validuser = TRUE;
  break;
default: error("fatal:sh - unknown login reply.\n");
  break;
  }
 }
 if (!validuser) { /* hangup. */
  print("Invalid access, sorry.\n");
  hangup();  /* force hangup. */
 } 
/* User is now validated. */
    user_ptr = &user_data_ptr[My_tty];
 strcpy_at_from_star(user_ptr->gl_username, username);
 set_user(@&user_ptr->gl_useracct);
 print("Login: %s,  Acct. %03d-%04d.\n", username, @&user_ptr->gl_useracct,
  @&user_ptr->gl_userindex);
 print("Terminal: tty%u, %04x.\n",My_tty, My_tid);
 taskid = create(1, BEQUEATH_TTY, My_priority, 0, 0,
  "/sys/menu", 0);
    error("warn:sh - failed to transform menu.  TID=%x04.\n",
  taskid);
}

/*  time - System time and date routines.
  83/09/25. A. E. Klietz.
  dateconv(bindate) - converts the binary date array bindate[2]
into a character string of the format:
   "83 Sep 25"
  timeconv(bindate) - converts the binary date array bindate[2]
into a character string of the format:
   "11:34:45 pm"
 
  date() - returns the date in a string as above.
  time() - returns the time in a string as above.
  secdiff(bindate1, bindate2) - returns the difference in
seconds of bindate2 - bindate1.  If > 18 hours, then it returns
65535.
  mindiff(bindate1, bindate2) - returns the difference in
minutes of bindate2 - bindate1.  If > 22 days, then it returns
31680 minutes.
  count_days(bindate) - returns the number of days since Jan 1st,
1980.
*/
#asm "seg 2"  /* force array into segment 2 (constants) */
static char months[13][4] {
 "???", "Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun",
 "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"
};
#asm "seg 2"
static int year_count[16] {
0, 366, 731, 1096, 1461, 1827, 2192, 2557, 2922, 3288, 3653, 4018, 4383, 
4749, 5114, 5479 };
#asm "seg 2"
static int day_count[2][12] {
0, 31, 59, 90, 120, 151, 181, 212, 243, 273, 304, 334,
0, 31, 60, 91, 121, 152, 182, 213, 244, 274, 305, 335 };
char *dateconv(d)                                 
unsigned d[]; /* binary date array [2] */
{                            
 static char datestr[12];
 strcpy(datestr,"           "); /* zero out old date. */
 tsprintf(datestr,"%u %s %u", (d[0] >> 12)+80, months[(d[0] >> 8) & 0xf],
  (d[
/*   iosubs - Standard input/output subroutines.
  83/10/01. A. E. Klietz.
  These are the input/output routines used by most
GamBit programs. 
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include "aekdef.h"
#include "globdef.h"
extern  byte  My_tty;
static int   *monflag,  montty;   /* addr of global mon flag, and local copy. */
static FILE  *monfp;   /* file pointer of monitoring tty */
int  parseloc; /* location of next character in buffer for parsing. */
clean_string(string)
/* Procedure clean_string - remove parity bit (high bit) and skip control
characters (other than tabs) */
char *string;
{
 register int i;
 register char ch;
 int  j, limit;
 i=0;
 while (string[i] &= 0x007F)   /* strip parity */
  i++;
 limit = i - 1; 
 i=j=0;
    for (i=0; i <= limit; i++)
  if ((ch = string[i]) >= ' ' && ch != 0x7F || ch == '\t')
   string[j++] = ch;
 string[j] = '\0'; /* ensure termination of string */
}
freadline(fp,string)
/* Procedure freadline - read a line of text from a file.
returns length of inputted line, -1 if end-of-file. */
FILE *fp;
char *string;
{
int  len;
 len = fgetstr(fp,string,BUFLEN);
 if (len > 0)
  clean_string(string);
 return(len);
}
readline(string)
/* Procedure readline - read a line of text from input. */
char *string;
{
 int  len;
 unsigned bindate[2];
 extern struct gl_user_datum *user_data_ptr;
 if (user_data_ptr == 0)
  error("fatal:readline - undefined user_data_ptr!\n");
 len = freadline(stdin, string);
 if (montty != 0) /* being monitored */
  tfprintf(monfp, "*%d> %s\n", My_tty, string);
 
 get_date(bindate);  /* update last cr for timeout check */
 @&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_binarylastcr[0] = bindate[0];
 @&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_binarylastcr[1] = bindate[1];
 return(len);
}
print(string, a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6)
/* Procedure print - send a line of text to output using tfprintf */
char *string;
int  a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6;
{
extern  struct gl_user_datum  *user_data_ptr;
int  ttynum;
char port_name[8];
 if (monflag == 0) { /* not initialized */
  if (user_data_ptr == 0)
   error("fatal:print - user_data_ptr undefined.\n");
  monflag = &user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_monitor;
    }
 tfprintf(stdout, string, a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6);
 if (montty != @monflag) { /* change in monitor status */
  montty = @monflag;
  if (montty == 0) /* end of monitoring */
   fclose(monfp);
  else { /* beginning of monitoring */
   strcpy_star_from_at(port_name, user_data_ptr[
    (montty == -1 ? 0 : montty)].gl_ttyname);
   monfp = fopen(port_name, "wv");
  }
 }
  
 if (montty != 0) {  /* being monitored! */
  tfprintf(monfp, "%d> ",My_tty);
  tfprintf(monfp, string, a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6);
 }
}
isseparator(ch)
/* Procedure isseparator  - return true if ch is one of the
characters " ,.?\n\t#" */
int ch;
{
 char *delim = " ,.?\n\t#";
 char    ch2;
 int  pos    = 0;
 while ((ch2 = delim[pos++]) != '\0')
  if (ch == ch2)
   return(1);
 
 return(0);
}

tablp;
char *username[];
{
 static BUFTYPE unbuf;
 BUFTYPE pwbuf, fbuf;
    boolean endfile;
 unsigned option_save;
 rewind(fp);
 print("Username: ");
 if (readline(fbuf) == EOF)
  exit(0);
 reset_parse();
 upper_parse(fbuf,unbuf);
 if (parse(fbuf,pwbuf) == 0) {
  option_save = get_option(stdin);
  set_option(stdin,option_save & ~ECHO); /* turn off echo */
  print("Password: ");
  if (readline(pwbuf) == EOF)
   exit(0);
  set_option(stdin,option_save);
  print("\n");
 }
 while (((endfile = freadline(fp, fbuf)) != EOF) && strcmp(fbuf, unbuf))
  /* search pw file for login name */
  ;
 if (endfile == EOF)
  return(FALSE); /* no such un */
 getc(fp); /* skip tab */
 
 if (freadline(fp, fbuf) == EOF)
  return(FALSE);
 *username = unbuf;
 return(equal(fbuf, pwbuf));
}

/*  menu - GamBit menu program.
  83/10/01.  A. E. Klietz.
  'menu' is a program that handles all introductory communication
with the user.
  If argc > 1 then skip the sysnote and other login checks.
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include <dev.h>
#include "aekdef.h"
#include "globdef.h"
#include "validef.h"
#include "appdef.h"
#define BEQUEATH_TTY 0x04
#define EXC_BREAK 2
#define IDLE   0x0004
extern unsigned My_tid, Dads_tid;
extern char My_tty, User_num;
extern int  Error_status;
struct gl_user_datum  *user_data_ptr;
struct gl_system_datum  *system_data_ptr;
#asm "seg 2" /* force into constant data segment */
static char  cmdnames[][12] = {
 "bye        ",
 "clock      ",
 "control    ",
 "credit     ",
 "diplomacy  ",
 "goodbye    ",
 "help       ",
 "list       ",
 "mail       ",
 "news       ",
 "password   ",
 "scepter    ",
 "send       ",
 "set_option ",
 "talk       ",
 "tasks      ",
 "users      ",
 "who        "
};
#asm "seg 2"   /* force into constant data segment */
static struct cmdlist_type cmdlist[] = {
 0, 1,
 0, 15,
 0, 13,
 0, 12,
 0, 2,
 0, 1,
 0, 3,
 0, 11,
 0, 4,
 0, 5,
 0, 16,
 0, 6,
 0, 17,
 0, 14,
 0, 7,
 0, 8,
 0, 9,
 0, 10
};
#define CMDLIST_LEN (sizeof(cmdlist) / sizeof(struct cmdlist_type))
main(argc, argv)
int  argc;
char *argv[];
{
BUFTYPE buffer;
int  len;
FILE *fp;
extern break_handler();
boolean first_cmd = TRUE;
 
 unbreakable(); /* disable breaks here. */
 set_glob();    /* set global pointers */
 
 if (argc <= 1) { /* if initial call to menu. */
  if (Dads_tid == IDLE) /* dont change console's app */
   @&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_apnum = AP_MENU;
  print("                _\n");
  print("Welcome to the GamBit Menu.\n");
  if ((fp = fopen("/sys/note","r")) > 0) { /* show sysnote. */
   while (freadline(fp,buffer) != EOF)
    print("%s\n",buffer);
         fclose(fp);
  }
  
  if (!(@&system_data_ptr->gl_sysopen))
   if ((unsigned) User_num != 255) {
    print("System closed, sorry.\n");
    exit(0);
   }
   else
    print("(System closed for non-staff.)\n");
  print(" \n");
  print("Type 'help' for help.\n");
 } 
 else
  if (Dads_tid == IDLE) /* console does not issue sendap's */
   sendap(AP_MENU);
 for (len=0; len < CMDLIST_LEN; len++) /* set pointers in cmdlist */
  cmdlist[len].cmd = cmdnames[len];
 exc_handler(EXC_BREAK, 0, break_handler);
     if(!setexit()) {
        len = char_waiting(stdin);
        if (len > 0) {
      for (len=char_waiting(stdin);  len >= 1; len--)
         getchar(stdin);   /* skip bad parity characters */
        print("*INPUT ERR*\n");
      }
   else
      print("*INT*\n");
 }
 breakable(); /* enable breaks. */
 if (first_cmd) { /* list commands only on first iteration of cmd loop */
  first_cmd = FALSE;
  print("\n");
  list_cmds();
  print("\nType 'list' to list these commands.\n");
 }
  
 for (;;) {  /* loop forever. */
  do {
   print("\n");
   print("Enter menu command? ");
   len = readline(buffer);
   if (len == EOF) {  /* force a hangup command. */
    strcpy(buffer,"bye");
    len = 3;
   }
  } while (len == 0);
  if ((User_num == -1) && equal(buffer, "manager")) {
   if (Dads_tid == IDLE) 
    sendap(AP_SYSTEM); /* switch tables + journal entry */
   transform("/cmds/qnxsh", 0, 0);
  }
  docommand(buffer);
 }
}

{

 case 12: /* credits left */
   {

     @&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_startcredits - credit_left);
    print("and have %d credits remaining in\n",credit_left);

   break;

   if (User_num != -1)
    print("Command restricted, sorry.\n");

   break;
 case 14: /* set command */
{
 struct gl_user_datum *user_ptr;
   user_ptr = &user_data_ptr[My_tty];
   if (User_num == 1) { /* guest account */
    print("Sorry, the guest account cannot change its terminal parameters.\n");
    return(-1);
   }
   while (parse(command,word) <= 0) {
    print("Select one of these options:\n");
    print("     backspace\n");
    print("     duplex\n");
    print("     echo_backspace\n");    
    print("     line_erase\n");    
    print("\nEnter set option? ");
          reset_parse();
    if (readline(command) <= 0)
     return(0);
   }
   if (subset(word, "backspace")) {
    print("Do you want to use\n");
    print("     1) backspace key (Control-H)\n");
    print("     2) delete key    (DEL)\n");
    print("for erasing a character?\n");
    print("\nSelect 1 or 2 ? ");
    readline(command);
    if ((i = atoi(command)) < 1 || i > 2) {
     print("Incorrect value.\n");
     return(-1);
    }
    else
     @&user_ptr->gl_backspace =
      (i == 1) ? 0x08 : 0x7F;   
   } else if (subset(word, "duplex")) {   
    print("Do you want to set\n");
    print("     1) full duplex\n");
    print("     2) half duplex\n");
    print("\nSelect 1 or 2 ? ");
    readline(command);
    if ((i = atoi(command)) < 1 || i > 2)  {
     print("Incorrect value.\n");
     return(-1);
    }
    else {
     @&user_ptr->gl_ttyoptions &= ~ECHO;
     @&user_ptr->gl_ttyoptions |= (i == 1 ? ECHO : 0);
    }
   } else if (subset(word, "echo_backspace")) {
    print("Do you want\n");
    print("     1) Backspaces echoed with space/back/space\n");
    print("     2) No special echoing of backspaces\n");
    print("\nSelect 1 or 2 ? ");
    readline(command);
    if ((i = atoi(command)) < 1 || i > 2) {
     print("Incorrect value.\n");
     return(-1);
    }
    else {
     @&user_ptr->gl_ttyoptions &= ~EDEL;
     @&user_ptr->gl_ttyoptions |= (i == 1 ? EDEL : 0);
    }
   } else if (subset(word, "line_erase")) {
    print("Select the character you wish to use for erasing a line:\n");
    print("     1) Control-U  (NAK)\n");
    print("     2) Control-X  (CAN)\n");
    print("     3) Escape key (ESC)\n");
    print("\nSelect 1, 2, or 3 ? ");
    readline(command);
    if ((i = atoi(command)) < 1 || i > 3) {
     print("Incorrect value.\n");
     return(-1);
    }
    else {
     switch (i) {
  case 1: i = 0x15;
    break;
  case 2: i = 0x18;
    break;
  case 3: i = 0x1B;
    break;
  }
     @&user_ptr->gl_linedel = i;
    }
   } else {
    print("No such option.\n");
    return(-1);
   }
         set_glob();  /* set up new parameters */
   print("Option set to new value.\n");
   break;
}
    
 case 15:  /* clock */
   print("%s, %s.  %s time.\n", time(), date(),
    (@&system_data_ptr->gl_peakflag ? "Peak" : "Non-peak"));
   { unsigned bindate[2];
    bindate[0] = @&system_data_ptr->gl_binaryuptime[0];
    bindate[1] = @&system_data_ptr->gl_binaryuptime[1];
    print("System up at %s, %s.\n", timeconv(bindate),
     dateconv(bindate));

   break;

   if (User_num == 1) {
    print("Sorry, the guest account cannot change its password.\n");
    return(-1);
   }
   print("Enter old password? ");
   readline(word);
   reset_parse();
   parse(word,word);
   hashpassword(word, word);
   {
    struct va_user_record user_record;
    
    if (read_valid(@&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_userindex,
     &user_record) == 0) 
     print("Bad validuz read.\n");
    else 
     if (!equal(user_record.va_hashpw, word))
      print("Improper password.\n");
     else {
      BUFTYPE word2;
      print("Enter new password? ");
      readline(word);
      print("Enter new password again? ");
      readline(word2);
                        if (!equal(word, word2))
       print("Passwords didn't match.\n");
      else {
       reset_parse();
       parse(word, word);
       word[11] = '\0';
       if (strlen(word) < 4)
        print("Passwords must be at least 4 characters long.\n");
       else {
        hashpassword(word, user_record.va_hashpw);
           if (write_valid(user_record.va_userindex,
          &user_record) == 0)     
         print("Bad validuz write.\n");     
        else        
         print("Password changed.\n");         
       }
      }
     }
   }
            break;
 case 9: /* users */
   print("\nList of all users:\n");
   {
    FILE *fp;

    fiseek(fp, 256);  /* skip hash table */

     print("%-11s ",name);
     if (++i % 3 == 0)

 case 17: /* send msg to user */
 {
   extern  int  parseloc;

   }

   while (len == 0) { /* end of line */
    print("Enter message to send? ");
    len = readline(command);
    reset_parse();
   }
   while (isseparator(command[parseloc]))
    parseloc++;  /* skip leading spaces */
   for (i=0; i<NUMDEVICES && ttynum == -1; i++) {
    strcpy_star_from_at(name, user_data_ptr[i].gl_username);    
    string_tolower(name);

     ttynum = i;
   }
   if (ttynum < 0) {
    print("No such user logged in.\n"); 
    return(-1);
    }
   strcpy_star_from_at(tty_name, user_data_ptr[ttynum].gl_ttyname);
   fp = fopen(tty_name, "wv");
   strcpy_star_from_at(word, user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_username);
   tfprintf(fp, "\n\00_
user_record.va_userindex,
  &writemsg.ac_user_record);
 acct_reply(tid, &writemsg_reply, sizeof(writemsg_reply));

}
do_creditmsg(tid, msg_buf)
/* Procedure do_creditmsg - change user's credit balance. */
unsigned tid;
char  msg_buf[];
{
 struct ac_creditmsg   creditmsg;
 struct ac_creditmsg_reply  creditmsg_reply;
 struct va_user_record  user_record;
 int  oldcr, addcr;
 copy(&creditmsg, msg_buf, sizeof(creditmsg));
 if (valid_read(creditmsg.ac_userindex,
  &user_record) == 0)
  creditmsg_reply.ac_credit_success = 0; /* failed */
 else {
  oldcr = user_record.va_creditbalance;   
  addcr = creditmsg.ac_credits;
  if (abs(oldcr) + abs(addcr) < max(abs(oldcr), abs(addcr))) {
   creditmsg_reply.ac_credit_success = 2; /* overflow */
   acct_reply(tid, &creditmsg_reply, sizeof(creditmsg_reply));
  }
  else {
   creditmsg_reply.ac_credit_success = 1; /* success */   
   user_record.va_creditbalance = oldcr + addcr;
   if (valid_write(creditmsg.ac_userindex, &user_record) <= 0)
    error("fatal:acct - bad credit validuz write.\n");
   creditmsg_reply.ac_creditbalance = oldcr + addcr;
   acct_reply(tid, &creditmsg_reply, sizeof(creditmsg_reply));
   write_journal(CREDIT_ENTRY, creditmsg.ac_userindex, addcr);
  }
 }
}

  journal_record.jr_credits = apnum; /* apnum = added credits */
  journal_record.jr_apnum = 0;
  journal_record.jr_ttynum = ttynum; /* ttynum = userindex */

#include <stdio.h>
/*
 * hanoi
 */
#define FORMFEED  0x0c
#define ESC    0x1b
#define POST   0xba
#define POST_BASE  0xca
#define BASE   0xcd
#define RING   0xdc
#define SCREEN_WIDTH 80
#define SCREEN_HEIGHT 25
#define RING_WIDTH  ((((SCREEN_WIDTH - 2)/3) & 0xfe)-1)
#define LEFT_POST  (RING_WIDTH/2+1)
#define CENTER_POST  (LEFT_POST+RING_WIDTH)
#define RIGHT_POST  (LEFT_POST+2*RING_WIDTH)
#define MOVING_ROW  2
#define BASE_ROW  15
#define POST_HEIGHT  11
char top[3] { BASE_ROW-1, BASE_ROW-1, BASE_ROW-1 };
int pause 0;
main(argc, argv)
int argc;
char *argv[];
 {
 int nrings;
 if(argc < 1  ||  argc > 3)
  abort("Use: hanoi [rings [delay]]\n");
 nrings = argc > 1 ? atoi(argv[1]) : 5;
 pause = argc > 2 ? atoi(argv[2]) : 1;
 setup(nrings);
 hanoi(nrings, 0, 2, 1);
 curse(0, SCREEN_HEIGHT-1);
 }
hanoi(n, a, b, c)
char n, a, b, c;
 {
 if(n == 0)
  return;
 hanoi(n-1, a, c, b);
 movering(n, a, b);
 hanoi(n-1, c, b, a);
 }
setup(n)
char n;
 {
 char i;
 putchar(FORMFEED);
 for(i = MOVING_ROW+2; i < BASE_ROW; ++i) {
  cput(LEFT_POST, i, POST);
  cput(CENTER_POST, i, POST);
  cput(RIGHT_POST, i, POST);
  }
 curse(0, BASE_ROW);
 for(i = 1; i < SCREEN_WIDTH; ++i)
  putchar(BASE);
 cput(LEFT_POST, BASE_ROW, POST_BASE);
 cput(CENTER_POST, BASE_ROW, POST_BASE);
 cput(RIGHT_POST, BASE_ROW, POST_BASE);
 for(i = n; i > 0; --i)
  draw(i, LEFT_POST, top[0]--, RING);
 }
curse(x, y)
char x, y;
 {
 putchar(ESC);
 putchar('=');
 putchar(y + ' ');
 putchar(x + ' ');
 }
cput(x, y, ch)
char ch;
int x,y;
 {
 putchar(ESC);
 putchar('=');
 putchar(y + ' ');
 putchar(x + ' ');
 putchar(ch);
 }
draw(ring, centre, y, ch)
char ring, centre, y, ch;
 {
 char i;
 curse(centre-ring, y);
 for(i=0; i<ring; ++i)
  putchar(ch);
 curse(centre+1, y);
 for(i=0; i<ring; ++i)
  putchar(ch);
 }
movering(ring, from, to)
char ring, from, to;
 {
 char fromc, toc;
 char fromy, toy;
 fromc = LEFT_POST + from * RING_WIDTH;
 toc = LEFT_POST + to * RING_WIDTH;
 fromy = ++top[from];
 toy = top[to]--;
 while(fromy != MOVING_ROW) {
  draw(ring, fromc, fromy, ' ');
  draw(ring, fromc, --fromy, RING);
  wait();
  }
 if(fromc < toc)
  while(fromc != toc) {
   cput(fromc-ring, fromy, ' ');
   cput(fromc, fromy, RING);
   cput(fromc+1, fromy, ' ');
   cput(fromc+ring+1, fromy, RING);
   ++fromc;
   wait();
   }
 else if (fromc > toc)
  while(fromc != toc) {
   cput(fromc+ring, fromy, ' ');
   cput(fromc, fromy, RING);
   cput(fromc-1, fromy, ' ');
   cput(fromc-ring-1, fromy, RING);
   --fromc;
   wait();
   }
 while(fromy != toy) {
  draw(ring, fromc, fromy, ' ');
  draw(ring, fromc, ++fromy, RING);
  wait();
  }
 }
wait() {
  fflush(stdout);
  time_slice();
 }

previous entries on the line.  The previous entries are stored in the
string array "depthlist"  */

{
 int i;
 print("\n");
 if (depth == 0) 
  print("Select topic? ");
 else {
  print("Select ");
  for(i=0; i<depth; i++)
   print("%s ",&depthlist[i]);
        print("subtopic? ");
 }
}
" QUIT\n");
 parseloc = temp;
}

do_constmsg(tid, msg_buf, sysnam, sysloc)
/* procedure constmsg - system constants. */
unsigned tid;
char  msg_buf[], *sysnam, *sysloc;
{
 struct ac_constmsg   constmsg;
 struct ac_constmsg_reply constmsg_reply;
 copy(&constmsg, msg_buf, sizeof(constmsg));
 strcpy(constmsg_reply.ac_sysnam, sysnam);
 strcpy(constmsg_reply.ac_sysloc, sysloc);
 reply(tid, &constmsg_reply, sizeof(constmsg_reply));
}

 reply_msg->ac_checkword = AC_CHECKWORD;
 reply(tid, reply_msg, size_reply_msg);

/*  help - GamBit help menu.
  83/10/01.  A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include "aekdef.h"
#define MAXHELPDEPTH 5
dohelp(command,root)
/* Procedure dohelp - process help command.  This procedure pulls
more parameters off the command buffer.  Each parameter is a help
entry in a help-tree.  Each node in the tree contains a list of sub-nodes
to further help entries.  Format for a help file is:
parameter1,   /help/file_parm1
parameter2,   /help/file_parm2
moreinfo,     /help/stuff
<blank line>
Short description....
*/
char *command, *root;
{
    int  return_status = -2;
 BUFTYPE depthlist[MAXHELPDEPTH]; /* list of nested help topic names. */
 FILE *helpfp;
while(return_status == -2)   /* "help" entered again. */
 return_status = nexthelp(command, root, depthlist, 0);
 print("You are leaving the help directory.\n");
}
nexthelp(command, filename, depthlist, depth)
    
char *command, *filename;
BUFTYPE *depthlist;
int  depth;  /* recursion depth. */
{
 BUFTYPE  newname, buffer;
 boolean  firstime = TRUE;
 int   len, temp, return_status;
 extern int parseloc;  /* next position in parse routines for search. */
 FILE  *helpfp;
if (depth >= MAXHELPDEPTH) { /* nested too deep. */
 print("help:warn - help recursion nested too deep.\n");
 return(-1);
}
if ((helpfp = fopen(filename,"rv")) == 0) 
 return(0);
 
return_status = 0;
while(return_status == 0) 
 if (parse(command,newname) == 0) { /* bottom of recursion. */
  if (firstime) { /* display entire help file. */
   rewind(helpfp);
   do {
    len = freadline(helpfp, buffer);
   } while (len > 0);  /* skip arg list. */
  
   print("\n");
   while (freadline(helpfp,buffer) != EOF) /* display help entry. */
    print("%s\n",buffer);
  }
  rewind(helpfp);
  freadline(helpfp, buffer);
  temp = parseloc;
  reset_parse();
  if (parse(buffer, buffer) > 0) { /* if args, then prompt. */
/* KLUDGE: If !brief || firstime then... */
/* pass 'buffer' as access to free string space. buffer destroyed */
    print_help_parms(helpfp, depth, buffer);
   firstime = FALSE;
   print_help_prompt(depthlist, depth);
   reset_parse();
       if ((len = readline(command)) <= 0) { /* go up to previous level. */
    fclose(helpfp);
    return(0);
   }
  }   
  else {
   parseloc = temp;
   firstime = FALSE;
   fclose(helpfp);
   return(0);
  }
 }
 else { /* descend to next recursion level. */
  firstime = FALSE;
  rewind(helpfp);
  temp = parseloc;
  do { /* scan for match to 'newname' */
   freadline(helpfp, buffer); /* buffer used as temp */
   reset_parse();
   len = upper_parse(buffer, buffer); /* get topic */
   strcpy(&depthlist[depth], buffer);
   string_tolower(buffer); /* buffer used as temp variable. */
   if (subset(newname, buffer))
    break;
         } while (len > 0);
  parse(buffer, buffer); /* now read in file name. */
  parseloc = temp;
  if (len <= 0)  { /* no match found. */
   if (!subset(newname,"back")) 
    if (subset(newname,"quit")) {
     fclose(helpfp);
     return(-1); /* abort */
    }
    else 
     if (subset(newname,"help")) {
      fclose(helpfp);
      return(-2); /* flag dohelp to loop again. */
     }
     else {
      print("\"%s\" is not one of the available options.\n",newname);
/* KLUDGE: if brief then do...     print_help_parms(helpfp, depth); */
      reset_parse();
      command[0] = '\0'; /* trash remainder of command line. */
     }
   else {
             fclose(helpfp);
    return(0); /* "back" */
   }
  }
  else { /* open new file. */
   print("\n");
     for (len=1; len <= depth; len++)
    print("   ");         
   print("%s\n",&depthlist[depth]);
   fclose(helpfp);
   return_status = nexthelp(command, buffer, depthlist, depth + 1);
   if (return_status < 0)
    return(return_status); /* pass the buck */
   if ((helpfp = fopen(filename,"rv")) == 0)
    return(return_status);
  }
 }
 fclose(helpfp);
 return(return_status);
}
print_help_parms(helpfp, depth, buffer)
/* Procedure print_help_parms - display the topic parameters in the given
help file.  */
FILE *helpfp;
int  depth;
char *buffer;
{
 int   num,len,temp;
 extern int parseloc;  /* location of parser pointer (iosubs) */
 rewind(helpfp);
 temp = parseloc;
 num = 0;
 print("\n");
 if (depth == 0)
  print("Please choose one of the following topics:\n");
 else
  print("Choose one of the following subtopics:\n");
 do { /* display all parameters */
  freadline(helpfp, buffer);
  reset_parse();
  len = upper_parse(buffer, buffer);
  if (len > 0) { /* display param */
   if (num % 5 == 0)
    print("\n");
   num++;
   print(" %-11s",buffer);
  }
 } while (len > 0);

/*  stringsubs - string process subroutines.
  83/10/01.  A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include "aekdef.h"
subset(part, full)
/* Procedure subset - returns TRUE if "part" is a substring
of "full". */
char *part, *full;
{
 char ch;
 while ((ch = *part++) == *full++ && ch != '\0')
  ;
 return(ch == '\0'); 
}
binary_wordmatch(word, wordlist, wordlist_len)
/* Procedure binary_wordmatch scans a alphabetized list for
a unique match and returns the hash code for the word from
the list.  A -1 is returned if not unique, and it issues 
an appropriate error message.  A 0 is returned is not found,
and the calling procedure must issue its own message. */
char *word;
struct  cmdlist_type  wordlist[];
int  wordlist_len;
{
int  diff, lower, upper, pos;
 if (word[0] == '\0')
  return(0);
 lower = 0;
 upper = wordlist_len - 1;
 do {
  pos = (lower + upper) / 2;
  diff = strcmp(wordlist[pos].cmd, word);       
  if (diff <= 0)
   lower = pos + 1;
  if (diff >= 0)
   upper = pos - 1;
  } while (lower <= upper);
 if (equal(wordlist[pos].cmd,word))
  return(wordlist[pos].cmdnum);
 if (!subset(word, wordlist[pos].cmd)) {
  pos++;
  if (pos > wordlist_len - 1 || !subset(word, wordlist[pos].cmd))
   return(0);
 }
 
 if (pos < wordlist_len - 1)
  if (subset(word, wordlist[pos + 1].cmd))
   return(-1); /* not unique error. */
 return(wordlist[pos].cmdnum);
}
pper) / 2;
  diff = strcmp(wordlist[pos].cmd, word);       
  if (diff <= 0)
   lower = pos + 1;
  if (diff >= 0)
   upper = pos - 1;
  } while (#

TP
 
     The  redo  keys  will let you re-enter the last command
you typed.  Pressing  control-F  and  "0"  will  recall  the
previous line.   Pressing control-F and "9" will execute the

     Here is quick summary of all the special keys that the
GBMS system recognizes:
     BACKSPACE or DEL  - delete the previous character.
     Control-C         - return you to the "top" level
                         of a program.
     Control-S         - pause the display.
     Control-Q         - resume the display.
     Control-U         - erase the current line.
     Control-F, 0      - recall the previous line.
     Control-F, 9      - recall and execute the previous line.
-
     To log in to GBMS you need a remote terminal with a
phone attachment.  GBMS talks over the phone line at 300
baud (30 characters per second).  You should set your
terminal to no parity, 8 data bits, and 1 stop bit.
 
     NOTE: You must use no parity.  Even parity, or odd
parity will not work.
E. Klietz.

     The  'help' command shows you a list of choices (called
"subtopics") that you can choose from.  Each subtopic gives 
you detailed information about  a  particular  aspect  of  a
topic.  You can find complete information about any topic by
simply reading all the subtopics.
     There are two special choices you can make at any time
instead of a subtopic.  These are:
     BACK - Go back up to the previous topic.  If you are already
            at the highest topic then you will go back to the 
            main menu.
     QUIT - Quit the help directory and go back to the main menu.
     You can enter these two choices at any time instead of chosing
a subtopic.

/*  set_glob - set global user and system pointers.
  83/10/30. A. E. Klietz.
 This library routine obtains the global user and system pointers
from the accounting administrator.  They are returned in the
variables user_data_ptr and system_data_ptr.   It also sets the
extra segment pointer to point to the global data tables.
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include <dev.h>
#include "aekdef.h"
#include "globdef.h"
#include "ac_msgs.h"
extern  struct gl_system_datum  *system_data_ptr;
extern struct gl_user_datum  *user_data_ptr;
extern  byte    My_tty;
set_glob()
{
 struct ac_globmsg   globmsg;
 struct ac_globmsg_reply globmsg_reply;
 struct  dev_entry buffer;
 globmsg.ac_checkword = AC_CHECKWORD;
 globmsg.ac_msgnum  = GLOBMSG;
 if (send(detach(ACCOUNTING_PORT), &globmsg, &globmsg_reply,
   sizeof(globmsg)) <= 0)
  error("fatal:get_glob - unable to send to accounting admin.\n");
 if (globmsg_reply.ac_checkword != AC_CHECKWORD)
  error("fatal:get_glob - bad ac_checkword.\n");
 set_extra_seg(globmsg_reply.ac_global_seg_addr);
 system_data_ptr = globmsg_reply.ac_system_data_ptr;
 user_data_ptr = globmsg_reply.ac_user_data_ptr;
/*  set_term_config - set terminal configuration based on 
gl_ttyoptions, gl_backspace, and gl_linedel. */
 get_attr(stdin, &buffer);
 if (@&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_userindex == 0)
  return(0); /* dont change tty settings */
 buffer.tty_options = @&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_ttyoptions;
 if (buffer.tty_type == DEV_ASCII) /* if dial up port */
  buffer.tty_RUBOUT = @&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_backspace;
 buffer.tty_CANCEL = @&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_linedel;
 set_attr(stdin, &buffer);
}
ser_data_ptr;
/*  set_term_config - set terminal configuration based on 
gl_ttyoptions, gl_backspace, and gl_linedel. */
 get_attr(stdin, &buffer);
 if (@&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_userindex == 0)
  return(0); /* dont change tty settings */
 buffer.tty_options = @&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_ttyoptions;
 if (buffer.tty_type == DEV_ASCII) /* if dial up port */
  buffer.tty_RUBOUT = @&user_Z

per_parse(fbuf,namebuf);
 namebuf[11] = '\0';  /* truncate to 11+eos characters. */
 strcpy(username, namebuf);
 string_tolower(namebuf); /* convert to lowercase. */
 strcpy(loginmsg.ac_username, namebuf);
 if (parse(fbuf,namebuf) == 0) {
  option_save = get_option(stdin);
  set_option(stdin,option_save & ~ECHO); /* turn off echo */
  print("Password: ");
  if (readline(namebuf) == EOF)
   exit(0);
  set_option(stdin,option_save);
  print("\n");
 }
 string_tolower(namebuf);
 namebuf[11] = '\0';  /* truncate to 11+eos characters. */
 strcpy(loginmsg.ac_userpw, namebuf);
 if (send(detach(ACCOUNTING_PORT), &loginmsg, &loginmsg_reply,
  sizeof(loginmsg)) <= 0)
   error("fatal:sh - accounting admin failure.\n");
 return(loginmsg_reply.ac_login_success);
}
print_renew_msg()
/* procedure print_renew_msg */
{
  print("To renew your account, please mail a check or money order\n");
  print("to:\n\n");
  print("     GamBit Multi Systems, Inc.\n"); 
  print("     P.O. Box 23495\n");
  print("     Minneapolis, MN  55423\n\n");
  print("(Be sure to write your codename somewhere on the check.)\n");
  print("If you have already renewed your account, then please dis-\n");
  print("regard this notice.  Thank you.\n\n");
}
hangup()
/* Procedure hangup - send hangup exception to task. */
{
 set_exception(My_tid, 0x0001, 0);
}
trans_QNXshell(argc, argv)
/* Procedure trans_QNXshell - convert task to a standard shell.  Any
arguments sent to this shell are passed to the QNX shell */
int  argc;
char *argv[];
{
 unsigned taskid;
 int  i;
 argv[argc] = NULL;
#ifdef DEBUG
 tfprintf(stderr,"qnxsh.\n");
#endif
 
 taskid = create(1, (tty_tid(My_tty) == My_tid) ? BEQUEATH_TTY : RUN_CONCURRENT, 
   My_priority, 0, 0, "/cmds/qnxsh",
  argv[1], argv[2], argv[3], argv[4], argv[5], argv[6], argv[7],
  argv[8], argv[9], argv[10], 0);
 tfprintf(stderr,"warn:sh - failed to transform to qnxsh. TID=%x\n",taskid);
 exit(-1);
}

   break;
 }
 default:
   print("warn:menu - no such command index!\n");
   break;
 }
}
list_cmds()
/* list available commands */
{
  int  i, skip, index;
  print("The available menu commands are:\n");
  {
   skip = (CMDLIST_LEN / 3) + 1;
   for (i=0; i < skip * 3; i++) {
    if (i % 3 == 0)
     print("\n");
    index = (i % 3)*skip + i/3;
    if (index < CMDLIST_LEN)
     print("%-11s",cmdlist[index].cmd);
   }
  }
  print("\n");
}

and input gating on the terminal so we don't choke the monitor on an
i/o block.  (argh!)     set_stty will reset the parameters correctly. */
 get_attr(fw,&buffer);
 if (buffer.tty_type == DEV_ASCII) {
  buffer.tty_ESCAPE = 0x0000; /* disable shell escape. */
  term->iobase = buffer.tty_ioport;
 }
 else {
  buffer.tty_ESCAPE = 0x001A; /* reset escape to cntl-z */
  term->iobase = 0;
 }
 set_attr(fw,&buffer);
 set_option(fw, get_option(fr) & ~ECHO & ~EDIT);
 tfprintf(fw,"%cAT M0E0Q1S0=1S10=10S2=21%c\n",CR,CR); /* initialize Hayes modem. */
 fflush(fw);             
 sleep(5); /* give modem a chance to initialize. */
 while (char_waiting(fr))
  getc(fr); /* flush input buffer */
 fclose(fw);
 fclose(fr);
 tprintf("info:monitor - %s initialized.\n",term->name);
}
send_accounting_logoff(term)
/* Procedure send_accounting_logoff - send a message to the
 Accounting Administrator saying the terminal hung up. */
struct termlist_type *term;
{
    struct ac_logoutmsg logoutmsg;
 struct ac_logoutmsg_reply logoutmsg_reply;
 logoutmsg.ac_checkword = AC_CHECKWORD;
 logoutmsg.ac_msgnum   = LOGOUTMSG;
 logoutmsg.ac_ttynum   = term->ttynum;
 if (send(detach(ACCOUNTING_PORT), &logoutmsg, &logoutmsg_reply,
   sizeof(logoutmsg)) <= 0)
  error("fatal:monitor - unable to send to accounting admin.\n");
 if (logoutmsg_reply.ac_logout_success != 1) /* bad logout. */
  tprintf("note:monitor - bad logout request for tty%d.\n",term->ttynum);
}
clear_monitorflag(port)
int  port;
/* procedure clear_monitorflag - reset monitor flags to zero for all
other ports being monitored by this port. */
{
 register  i;
 int    *monflag;
 port = (port == 0 ? -1 : port);
 for (i=0; i< NUMDEVICES; i++) {
  monflag = &user_data_ptr[i].gl_monitor;
  if (@monflag == port)
   @monflag = 0; /* reset monitoring port to zero */
 }
}
check_timeout()
/* procedure check_timeout - check for 15 minute timeout. */
/* Also, check for peak/non-peak time and set flag as needed. */
{
 register  i;
 struct gl_user_datum  *user_ptr;
 unsigned peak, olddate[2], bindate[2];
 boolean peakchange;
 extern unsigned secdiff();
 get_date(bindate);
 peak = bindate[1] / 3600;
 if (bindate[0] & 0x01)
  peak += 12; /* 24 hour time format */
 peak = (peak < 2 || peak >= 12);  /* true if peak time. */
 peakchange = (peak != @&system_data_ptr->gl_peakflag);
 
 for(i=0; i < TERMLIST_LEN; i++)
  if (termlist[i].enabled) {
   user_ptr = &user_data_ptr[termlist[i].ttynum];
   if (@&user_ptr->gl_userindex != 0 &&  /* must be logged in */
     @&user_ptr->gl_apnum != AP_SYSTEM) { /* dont check system tasks*/
    if (peakchange)
     credit_update(termlist[i].ttynum, 0); 
    /* update credits before changing peak flag */
    olddate[0] = @&user_ptr->gl_binarylastcr[0];
    olddate[1] = @&user_ptr->gl_binarylastcr[1];
    if (secdiff(olddate, bindate) > 900) { /* fifteen minutes */
     set_exception(termlist[i].taskid, EXC_HANGUP, 0);
     tprintf("%s %s info:monitor - timeout on tty%d.\n",
      date(), time(), termlist[i].ttynum);
    }
   }
        }
 if (peakchange) {
  @&system_data_ptr->gl_peakflag = peak;
  print("%s %s info:monitor - start of %speak time.\n",
   date(), time(), (peak ? "" : "non-"));
 }
 get_date(olddate); /* set olddate to 4:40 am */
 olddate[0] &= ~0x01; /* force am */
 olddate[1] = 4*60*60 + 40*60; /* 4:40 */
 if (abs(mindiff(olddate, bindate)) <= 5 && !@&system_data_ptr->gl_warncrash) {
  @&system_data_ptr->gl_warncrash = TRUE;
  create(0, RUN_BACKGROUND, My_priority, 0, 0, "/cmds/qnxsh", "/config/down_script",
   0); /* create script to shut down system in 20 minutes. */
 }
}

  @&system_data_ptr->gl_warncrash = TRUE;
  create(0, RUN_BACKGROUND, My_priority, 0, 0, "/cmds/qnxsh", "/config/down_script",
   0); /* create scx

#include <stdio.h>
int   blah[256];
main()
{
 FILE *fp;
 fp = fopen("/val/valindx", "wv");
 fput(blah, 512, fp);
}
= 1; /* find open hole in /val/valindx */
 while ((numread = fget(&user_hash, 16, fp)) == 16 &&
   user_hash.vi_username[0] != '\0') 
  indexloc++;
 if (numread == 16)
  fiseek(fp, -16); /* backspace 1 record */
 
 if ((fp2 = fopen("/val/validuz", "rwv")) == 0) {
  fclose(fp);
  return(0);
 }
 recordloc = 1;
 while ((numread = fget(&user_record, 256, fp2)) == 256 && 
   user_record.va_userindex != 0)
  recordloc++;
 
 if (numread == 256)  /* found empty slot. */
  fiseek(fp2, -256); /* backspace one record. */
 zero(&user_record, sizeof(user_record));
 strcpy(user_record.va_username, username);
 user_record.va_userindex = recordloc;
 fput(&user_record, 256, fp2); /* write new record. */
 fclose(fp2);
 zero(&user_hash, sizeof(user_hash));
 strcpy(user_hash.vi_username, username);
 user_hash.vi_userindex = recordloc;
 user_hash.vi_link = 0;
 fput(&user_hash, 16, fp); 
 tableloc = hash_index(username);
 rewind(fp); 
 if (hashtable[tableloc] == 0) {  /* no collision */
  hashtable[tableloc] = indexloc;
  fput(hashtable, 512, fp); /* rewrite hash table */
 }
 else { /* collision */
  int  curloc;
  curloc = 0;
  hash_read(fp, &user_hash, hashtable[tableloc], &curloc);
  while (user_hash.vi_link != 0) 
   hash_read(fp, &user_hash, user_hash.vi_link, &curloc);   
  fiseek(fp, -16); /* backup 1 record */
  user_hash.vi_link = indexloc;  
  fput(&user_hash, 16, fp);
 }
 
 fclose(fp);
 return(recordloc);
 
}
unsigned valid_delete(userindex)
/* Procedure valid_delete - delete an entry from the valid file and
zero out its valindx entry. */
unsigned userindex;
{
 struct va_user_record user_record;
 struct vi_user_hash user_hash, user_hash2;
 FILE *fp;
 int  prevlink, curloc, hashindex, index;
 int     hashtable[256];
 
 if (valid_read(userindex, &user_record) == 0)
  return(0);
 
 hashindex = hash_index(user_record.va_username);
 index = user_record.va_userindex;
                                
 zero(&user_record, sizeof(user_record));
 if (valid_write(userindex, &user_record) == 0)
  return(0);
 if ((fp = fopen("/val/valindx","rwv")) == 0)
  return(0);
 
 if (fget(hashtable, 512, fp) != 512)
  error("valid:fatal - missing hash record in valindx!\n");
                  
 if (hashtable[hashindex] == 0)
  error("valid:fatal - hash entry is zero.\n");
 curloc = 512;
 if (hashtable[hashindex] == index) {
  hash_read(fp, &user_hash, index, &curloc);
  hashtable[hashindex] = user_hash.vi_link;
  rewind(fp);
  fput(hashtable, 512, fp);
  curloc = 512;
 }
 else {
  hash_read(fp, &user_hash, hashtable[hashindex], &curloc);
  prevlink = hashtable[hashindex];
  while (user_hash.vi_link != index) {
   prevlink = user_hash.vi_link;
   hash_read(fp, &user_hash, prevlink, &curloc);
  }
  hash_read(fp, &user_hash2, index, &curloc); 
  user_hash.vi_link = user_hash2.vi_link;
  hash_write(fp, &user_hash, prevlink, &curloc);
 }
 user_hash.vi_username[0] = '\0';
 user_hash.vi_userindex = 0;
 user_hash.vi_link = 0;
 hash_write(fp, &user_hash, index, &curloc);
 fclose(fp);
 return(userindex);  /* success */
}

/*  Globdef - Global memory definitions.
  83/10/20.  A. E. Klietz.
 This procedure outlines the global memory definitions.
The global memory area is allocated by /sys/accounting.  It's
location is sent to other tasks via an accounting request.
*/
/* This definition file is dependent on aekdef.h */
#define NUMDEVICES 24
typedef struct gl_user_datum {           
 char  gl_username[12];  /* name of user. */
 unsigned  gl_useracct;      /* user account number. (001-254) */
 unsigned    gl_userindex;      /* user index number. (0001-9999)  */
 unsigned gl_logintime[2];     /* time logged in (binary) */
 unsigned gl_last_creditupdate[2];   /* date/time credits updated. */  
 unsigned gl_binarylastcr[2];  /* time of last carriage return */
 int   gl_monitor;       /* monitoring terminal. -1 if none. */
 int    gl_creditbalance;    /* number of credits remaining. 
            (negative if in debt.) */
 char  gl_ttyname[8];   /* name of tty: $con2, $term10, etc. */
 unsigned gl_ttyoptions;       /* tty_option bits (echo, tabs, etc.)*/
 byte  gl_apnum;    /* current application. */
 byte  gl_dummy;    /* alignment dummy. */
 int   gl_startcredits;  /* starting credits at login. */
 int         gl_minute_remainder; /* internal credit_update counter */
    byte  gl_backspace;   /* backspace character. */
 byte  gl_linedel;    /* line-delete character. */
} GL_USER_DATA[NUMDEVICES];
typedef struct  gl_system_datum {
 unsigned gl_binaryuptime[2]; /* up time/date in binary. */
 boolean  gl_sysopen;  /

/*  Accounting - master GamBit accounting program.
  83/10/20. A. E. Klietz.
 This program receives accounting messages from all other tasks.
It issues the messages to the journal file (/jour/journal) as received.
 The accounting program also maintains the global memory area
(user_data and system_data).
*/
#define IDLE  0x0004
#define MSGBUFLEN 512
#include "aekdef.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include "globdef.h"
#include "validef.h"
#include "ac_msgs.h"
#include "appdef.h"
extern unsigned  My_tid, Dads_tid;
extern char   My_tty;
unsigned global_seg_addr;   /* addr of global segment. */
unsigned global_seg_size;   /* size of global segment. */
struct gl_system_datum *system_data_ptr;    /* pointer to global system data. */
struct gl_user_datum   *user_data_ptr;      /* pointer to global user data. */
main(argc, argv)
int  argc;
char *argv[];
{
 byte *zeroptr; /* pointer to zero out the global page. */
 char *version = "1.2";
 unsigned bindate[2], tid;
 char msg_buf[MSGBUFLEN];
 FILE *fp;
 char sysnam[12], sysloc[12];
 
 sleep(2); /* wait so we dont mix output with foreground task. */
 tprintf("\nGamBit Accounting Administrator ver %s.\n",version);
    if (argc == 0) { /*query*/
  tfprintf(stderr,"accounting & - initiate accounting administrator.\n");
  exit(0);
 }
 if (My_tid == tty_tid(My_tty))
  abort("fatal:acct - The acct administrator must be run as a backround task.\n");
 if (attach(ACCOUNTING_PORT) != 0)
  abort("fatal:acct - Another accounting task is already active!\n");
/*  KLUDGE - comment out for testing only!!!!
 if (Dads_tid != 0x0105)
  abort("fatal:acct - Must be initiated by /config/sys.init\n");
*/
 unbreakable(); /* no interruptions, please. */
 /* Now, allocate the global memory page for use by all tasks. */
    global_seg_size = (sizeof(GL_USER_DATA) + sizeof(GL_SYSTEM_DATA) + 1) / 16 + 1;
 
/* KLUDGE - alloc_perm_segment is not available!!!     KLUDGE  */
 global_seg_addr = alloc_segment(global_seg_size);
 
 if (global_seg_addr == 0)
  abort("fatal:acct - unable to allocate global memory page.\n"); 
 set_extra_seg(global_seg_addr);
 /* Zero out the global page. */
 for (zeroptr=0; zeroptr < global_seg_size * 16; zeroptr++) 
  @zeroptr = 0;  
 user_data_ptr = (struct gl_user_datum *) 1; /* offset by one to make it non-NULL (nonzero) */
 system_data_ptr = (struct gl_system_datum *) (sizeof(GL_USER_DATA) + 1); /* skip past user_data */
 /* Initialize system-wide variables in global page. */
 get_date(bindate);
 @&system_data_ptr->gl_binaryuptime[0] = bindate[0];
 @&system_data_ptr->gl_binaryuptime[1] = bindate[1];
 @&system_data_ptr->gl_sysopen = TRUE;
 @&system_data_ptr->gl_sysidle = FALSE;
 if ((fp = fopen("/config/sysconst","r")) <= 0)
  tprintf("caut:acct - no /config/sysconst file found.\n");
 else {
  freadline(fp, msg_buf);
  msg_buf[11] = '\0'; /* truncate to 11+eos */
  strcpy(sysnam, msg_buf);
  freadline(fp, msg_buf);
  msg_buf[11] = '\0'; 
  strcpy(sysloc, msg_buf);
  fclose(fp);
 }
/* Initialize terminal zero's global page entry. */
 strcpy_at_from_star(user_data_ptr->gl_username, "GamBit");
 @&user_data_ptr->gl_useracct = 255;
    @&user_data_ptr->gl_userindex = 9999;
 @&user_data_ptr->gl_logintime[0] = bindate[0];
 @&user_data_ptr->gl_logintime[1] = bindate[1];
 @&user_data_ptr->gl_last_creditupdate[0] = bindate[0];
 @&user_data_ptr->gl_last_creditupdate[1] = bindate[1];
 @&user_data_ptr->gl_creditbalance = 10000;
 strcpy_at_from_star(user_data_ptr->gl_ttyname, "$con");
 @&user_data_ptr->gl_ttyoptions = 0x00DF;
 @&user_data_ptr->gl_backspace = 0x7F;
 @&user_data_ptr->gl_linedel = 0x15;
 @&user_data_ptr->gl_apnum = AP_SYSTEM;
 @&user_data_ptr->gl_startcredits = 10000;
 
 tprintf("The Accounting Administrator is ready for operations.\n");
 for (;;) { /* loop forever */
  zero(msg_buf, MSGBUFLEN);  /* zero out the receive buffer. */
  breakable();  /* allow interrupts. */
  tid = receive(0, msg_buf, MSGBUFLEN);
  unbreakable(); /* disable interrupts. */
  if ((tid & 0xFF) == 0 || tid == -1)
   abort("fatal:acct - failed to receive message.\n");
  if (msg_buf[0] != AC_CHECKWORD) {
   tprintf("warn:acct - bad check byte on message from %04x.\n",tid);
  }
  else {
   switch (msg_buf[2]) {
 case GLOBMSG: /* request for global_seg_addr. */
  do_globmsg(tid, global_seg_addr);
  break;
 
    case LOGOUTMSG: /* message from /sys/monitor about a logout. */
  do_logoutmsg(tid, msg_buf);  
  break;
 case LOGINMSG: /* message from /cmds/sh about a login. */
  do_loginmsg(tid, msg_buf);
  break;
 case CONSTMSG: /* request for system version, location, etc. */
  do_constmsg(tid, msg_buf, sysnam, sysloc);
  break;
 case NEWAPMSG: /* new application started. */
  do_newapmsg(tid, msg_buf);
  break;
    case INTLOCKMSG: /* interlock accounting */
  do_intlockmsg(tid, msg_buf);
  break;
 case READMSG: /* read user record */
  do_readmsg(tid, msg_buf);
  break;
 case WRITEMSG: /* write user record */
  do_writemsg(tid, msg_buf);
  break;
 
 case CREDITMSG: /* update credit amount. */
  do_creditmsg(tid, msg_buf);
  break;
 default: /* no such message */
  tprintf("warn:acct - no such acct message, %d.\n",msg_buf[2]);  
  break;
   } /* end switch */  
  }  /* end else */
 }  /* end loop forever */
} /* end main. */

/*  xtra_strcpy - Extra string copy routines.
  83/10/20. A. E. Klietz.

strcpy_at_from_star(s, t) /* copy *t to @s. */
char *s, *t;
{
 while (@s++ = *t++)
  ;
}
strcpy_star_from_at(s, t) /* copy @t to *s. */
char *s, *t;
{
 while (*s++ = @t++)
  ;

/*  talk_cmds - talk commands.
  83/12/02. A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include "aekdef.h"
#include "multidef.h"
#include "talkextern.h"
do_command(command)
char *command;
/* procedure talk_cmds - main command execution routine. */
{
 reset_parse();
 switch (user->us_entry) {
case US_ENTERNAME:  getname(command);
     break;
case US_CMD:  do_cmd(command);
     break;
default:   errlog("No such command, %d.\n",user->us_entry);
     break;
 }
} 
getname(command)
/* getname - read name, test for uniqueness, and log in user. */
If command is zero, then set name to user_data_ptr name. */
{
 char  name[12], lowername[12];
 if (command[0] == '\0')
  strcpy_star_from_at(name,
   @&user_data_ptr[genuser[My_port].gn_ttynum].gl_username);
 else
  name = upper_parse(command, name);
 name[12] = '\0';
 strcpy(lowername, name);
 string_tolower(lowername);
 for (i=0; i < MAX_PORTS; i++)
  if (equal(lowername, user_array[i].us_lowername)) {
   mwrite(My_mask, "Duplicate name, try again.\nEnter your name? ");
   return(0);
  }
 strcpy(genuser[My_port].gn_name, name);
 strcpy(user->us_lowername, lowername);
 user->us_entry = US_CMD;
 user->us_echo = TRUE;
 user->tablenum = 0; /* main floor */
 mwrite(All_mask,"### %s just arrived in the %s on port #%d.  ",
  name, table_list[0], My_port); 
 mwrite(All_mask,"(%03u-%04u)\n", genuser[My_port].gn_usernum,
  genuser[My_port].gn_userindex));
  mwrite(My_mask,"Welcome to GamBit Talk.  Type '/help' for help.\n");
}
int match_user(name)
/* procedure match_user - return the port # matching the given name.
returns -1 if not found, -2 if not unique.  In both cases an error message
is returned to the user. */
char *name;
{
 int  count = 0, found = 0;
 if (name[0] == '\0') {
  mwrite(My_mask,"Missing name of user.\n");
  return(-1);
 }
    for (i=0; i<MAX_PORTS; i++)
  if (subset(name, user_array[i].us_lowername)) {
   found = i;
   count++; 
  }
 if (count == 0) { 
  mwrite(My_mask,"%s - no such user.\n",name);
  return(-1);
 }
 if (count == 1)
  return(found);
 mwrite(My_mask, "name is not unique.\n"); 
 return(-2);
}
int match_table(name)
/* procedure match_table - return table # matching given table.  
return -1 if table not found.  Search public tables for subsets of
name.  Seach private tables for full name. */
char *name;
{
 register i;
    
 if (name[0] == '\0') {
  mwrite(My_mask, "Missing name of table.\n");
  return(-1);
 }
 for (i=0; i < TABLE_LIST_LEN; i++)
  if (table_list[i].tb_public) {
   if (subset(name, table_list[i].tb_name)
    return(i);
  }
  else
   if (equal(name, table_list[i].tb_name)
    return(i);
 mwrite(My_mask, "%s - no such table name.\n",name);
}
 # matching given table.  
return -1 if table not found.  Search public tables for subsets of
name.  Seach private tables for full name. */
char *name;
{
 register i;
    
 if (name[0] == '\0') {
  mwrite(My_mask, "Missing name of table.\n");
  return(-1);
 }
 for (i=0; i < TABLE_LIST_LEN; i++)
  if (table_list[i].tb_public) {
   if (subset(name, table_list[i].tb_name)
    return(i);
  }
  else

/*  ac_msgs.h - Accounting message definitions.
  83/10/21. A. E. Klietz.
 The following is a list of standard message definitions for the
accounting administrator (/sys/accounting).
 This file is dependent on aekdef.h and globdef.h.
*/
#define AC_CHECKWORD   0x6C
#define ACCOUNTING_PORT  22
enum ac_msgnug_type {GLOBMSG, LOGINMSG, LOGOUTMSG, CONSTMSG,
 NEWAPMSG, INTLOCKMSG, READMSG, WRITEMSG, CREDITMSG, ACMAXMSG};
struct ac_generic_reply {   /* generic ac message */
 int   ac_checkword;
 char  ac_buffer[126];
};
struct ac_globmsg {
 int   ac_checkword;
 byte  ac_msgnum;
}; 
struct ac_globmsg_reply {
 int   ac_checkword;
 unsigned ac_global_seg_addr;
 struct user_datum *ac_user_data_ptr;  /* pointer to global user data array. */
 struct system_datum *ac_system_data_ptr;  /* pointer to global system data. */
};
struct ac_loginmsg {
 int   ac_checkword;
 byte  ac_msgnum;
 byte  ac_ttynum;
 char  ac_username[12];
 char  ac_userpw[12];
 char  ac_ttyname[8];
};
struct ac_loginmsg_reply {
 int   ac_checkword;
 byte  ac_login_success;  /* = 0 if bad pw, =1 if ok. =2 if no credits */
};                              /* = 3 if bad login request. 4 if closed, */
        /* = 5 if low on credits. */
struct ac_logoutmsg {
 int   ac_checkword; 
 byte  ac_msgnum;
 byte  ac_ttynum;
};

 byte  ac_logout_success;  /* = 0 if bad logout req.  = 1 if ok. */
};
struct ac_constmsg {

 byte  ac_msgnum;
};
struct ac_constmsg_reply {
 int   a
/*  validef.h - validation file layout.
  83/10/21. A. E. Klietz.
 This file defines the format of a record in the validation file.
(/val/validuz and /val/valindx)
 This file depends on aekdef.h.
 These definitions must match with those on globdef.h.
*/
#define   va_defined
struct va_user_record {
 char  va_username[12]; /* codename of user. */
 char  va_hashpw[12];  /* hashed password. */
 unsigned    va_useracct;  /* user account number. (6001-6254) */
 unsigned va_userindex;    /* user index number. (001-999) */
 unsigned  va_ttyoptions;      /* ECHO, TABS, etc. */
 int   va_creditbalance;   /* number of credits remaining. */
            /*  (negative if in debt.) */
 unsigned va_lastlogin[2];    /* date of last login. */
 boolean  va_closed;    /* true if account is closed. */
 unsigned va_birthdate;  /* birthdate of user. */
 byte  va_rating;   /* credit "rating" of user. 0-worst. 10-best. */
 byte  va_backspace;  /* desired backspace char. */
 byte  va_linedel;      /* desired line-delete char. */
 boolean  va_nocharge;  /* true if no charge to this acct. */
 char  va_stringbuf[192]; /* string buffer with 
         name
         1st addr line.
         2nd addr line.
         3rd addr line.
               phone
         hobbies/interests  */
 boolean  va_stateres;  /* true is user lives in state (tax) */
 boolean     va_anonymous;  /* true if user wants to be anonymous */
 byte  va_dummy[19];  /* dummy blank fill. */
};
/* va_user_record must be exactly* 256 characters long. */ 
/* 
/* Validsubs - validation file subroutines.
 83/10/21. A. E. Klietz.
 
 Hash table added  83/12/25. 
 This is a set of library procedures for manipulating the
/val/validuz and /val/valindx files.  
 The major routines contained are:
 valid_hash(username) - return userindex for username.
         returns 0 if not found.
 valid_read(userindex, &user_record) - read data into user_record.
      returns 0 if bad read.
 valid_write(userindex, &user_record) - write user_record to file.
      returns 0 if bad write.
 
 valid_create(username) - returns new userindex to open space on file.
      returns 0 if no room, 32767 if duplicate entry.
 valid_delete(userindex) - deletes the entry from valindx.
      returns 0 if already empty.
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include "aekdef.h"
#include "validef.h"
#define  HASHSEED   0xD16B
int hash_index(username)
char *username;
/* procedure hash_index - return a value from 0 to 255 based on 
'username' */
{
 
 int  hash;
 register i;
 hash = HASHSEED;  /* set inital value */  
 for (i=0; username[i] != '\0'; i++)   /* scramble */
  hash = ((hash * username[i]) >> 3);
 return(hash & 0xFF);  /* truncate to 8 bits. */
}
hash_read(fp, user_hash, indx, curloc)
FILE  *fp;
struct  vi_user_hash *user_hash;
int   indx, *curloc;
/* Procedure hash_read - read index-th record from valindx file.
File is assumed already open.  Curloc keeps track of the current 
file position. */
{
 register i;
 i = *curloc;
 *curloc = (indx-1)*16 + 512;
 fiseek(fp, *curloc - i);
 *curloc += 16;
 if (fget(user_hash, 16, fp) != 16) 
  error("valid:warn - hash index outside of file!\n");
}
hash_write(fp, user_hash, indx, curloc)
FILE  *fp;
struct  vi_user_hash *user_hash;
int   indx, *curloc;
/* Procedure hash_write - write index-th record from valindx file.
File is assumed already open.  Curloc keeps track of the current 
file position. */
{
 register i;
 i = *curloc;
 *curloc = (indx-1)*16 + 512;
 fiseek(fp, *curloc - i);
 *curloc += 16;
 if (fput(user_hash, 16, fp) != 16) 
  error("valid:warn - unable to write valindx record.\n");
}
unsigned valid_hash(username)
/* Procedure valid_hash - search the /val/valindx file for a userindex
   to match the given username.   Returns zero if not found. */
char *username;
{
 FILE *fp;  /* pointer to index file. */
 struct vi_user_hash user_hash;
 int   hashtable[256];
 int  hash, curloc;
 if (sizeof(user_hash) != 16) 
  error("valid:warn - user_hash record is not 16 length!\n");
 if (username[0] == '\0')
  return(0);  /* blank username */
 if ((fp = fopen("/val/valindx", "rv")) == 0)
  return(0);  /* unable to open valindx file. */
 if (fget(hashtable, 512, fp) < 512)  
  error("valid:fatal - missing hash record in valindx!\n");
 curloc = 512;
 hash = hashtable[hash_index(username)];
 if (hash == 0) {
  fclose(fp);
  return(0);
 }
 hash_read(fp, &user_hash, hash, &curloc);
 
 for (;;) {
  if (equal(user_hash.vi_username, username))
   break;
  if (user_hash.vi_link == 0) {
   fclose(fp);
   return(0); /* name not found */
  }
  hash_read(fp, &user_hash, user_hash.vi_link, &curloc);
 } 
 fclose(fp);
 if (user_hash.vi_userindex == 0) 
  error("valid:warn - user index is zero?\n");
 return(user_hash.vi_userindex);
}
unsigned valid_read(userindex, user_record)
/* Procedure valid_read - read in the user_record pointed by userindex. 
Returns zero if bad read or not found. */
unsigned userindex;
struct  va_user_record *user_record;  /* pointer to buffer. */
{
 FILE *fp;  /* pointer to valid file. */
 if (sizeof(struct va_user_record) != 256) 
  error("valid:warn - user_record is not 256 length!\n");
 if (userindex <= 0 || userindex >= 128) {
  tfprintf(stderr,"valid:warn - userindex out of bounds. ui=%d.\n",userindex);
  return(0);
 }
 if ((fp = fopen("/val/validuz","rv")) == 0)
  return(0);  /* file not found */
 if (fiseek(fp, (userindex-1)*256) != 0) {
  fclose(fp);
  tfprintf(stderr,"valid:warn - offset outside of valid file!\n");
  return(0);
 }
 if (fget(user_record, 256, fp) != 256) {
  fclose(fp);
  tfprintf(stderr,"valid:warn - user_record read past EOF.\n");
  return(0);
 }
 fclose(fp);
 return(1); /* signal good read. */
}
unsigned valid_write(userindex, user_record)
/* Procedure valid_write - write a user_record to the valid file at userindex. */
unsigned userindex;
struct     va_user_record *user_record; /* pointer to buffer */
{
 FILE *fp; /* pointer to valid file. */
    if (userindex <= 0 || userindex >= 128) {
  tfprintf(stderr,"valid:warn - userindex write out of bounds.\n");
  return(0);
 }
 if ((fp = fopen("/val/validuz", "rwv")) == 0)
  return(0);
 if (fiseek(fp, (userindex-1)*256) != 0) {  
  fclose(fp);
  tfprintf(stderr,"valid:warn - write offset outside of valid file!\n");
  return(0);
 }
 if (fput(user_record, 256, fp) != 256) {
  fclose(fp);
  tfprintf(stderr,"valid:warn - write error.\n");
  return(0);
 }
 fclose(fp);
 return(1); /* signal good write. */
}

/* Procedure valid_create - create an entry in the valindx file and
the valid file. Entries are inserted into a 512 byte hash table that
heads the /val/valindx file. */
char *username;
{
 FILE *fp, *fp2;
 struct va_user_record user_record;
 struct vi_user_hash user_hash;
 int  numread, tableloc, indexloc, recordloc;
 int    hashtable[256];

  return(32767);  /* blank name or duplicate name error. */

  return(0);
 if (fget(hashtable, 512, fp) != 512)
  error("valid:fatal - missing hash record in valindx!\n");

/* hashpassword 
 83/10/21. A. E. Klietz.
*/
hashpassword(pw,hashpw)
/* Procedure hashpw - hash password into gibberish. */
char *pw, *hashpw;
{
 
 int seed = 0xc73f;
 while (*hashpw++ = *pw++ * seed)

/*  Acct_subs - Accounting subroutines.
  83/10/20. A. E. Klietz.

#define MSGBUFLEN 512
#include "aekdef.h"

extern struct gl_system_datum *system_data_ptr;    /* pointer to global system data. */
extern struct gl_user_datum   *user_data_ptr;      /* pointer to global user data. */
do_globmsg(tid, global_seg_addr)
/* Procedure do_globmsg - process a req for global segment address. */
unsigned tid;
unsigned global_seg_addr;
{
 struct ac_globmsg_reply  globmsg_reply;
 globmsg_reply.ac_global_seg_addr = global_seg_addr;

}
do_logoutmsg(tid, msg_buf)
/* Procedure do_logoutmsg - write a logout journal entry, and zero the
 user's global page table. */
unsigned tid;
char  msg_buf[];
{
 struct  ac_logoutmsg  logoutmsg;
 struct ac_logoutmsg_reply  logoutmsg_reply;
 struct va_user_record  user_record;
 struct gl_user_datum  *user_ptr;
 byte ttynum;
 byte *zeroptr, *bound;                      
 unsigned userindex, credits_left, credit_diff;
 copy(&logoutmsg, msg_buf, sizeof(logoutmsg));
 ttynum = logoutmsg.ac_ttynum;
 if (ttynum == 0 || (@&user_data_ptr[ttynum].gl_userindex == 0)) 
  /* bad logout request. */
  logoutmsg_reply.ac_logout_success = FALSE;
 else { /* process logout */
  user_ptr = &user_data_ptr[ttynum];
     logoutmsg_reply.ac_logout_success = TRUE;
  userindex = @&user_ptr->gl_userindex;
  if (valid_read(userindex, &user_record) <= 0)
   error("fatal:acct - bad logout validuz read.\n");
  credits_left = credit_update(ttynum, -1);
   
  credit_diff = @&user_ptr->gl_startcredits -
   user_record.va_creditbalance;
  write_journal(LOGOUT_ENTRY, ttynum, 0);
        if (!user_record.va_nocharge) /* dont debit free accounts */
   user_record.va_creditbalance = credits_left - credit_diff;
  user_record.va_lastlogin[0] = @&user_ptr->gl_logintime[0];
  user_record.va_lastlogin[1] = @&user_ptr->gl_logintime[1];
  user_record.va_ttyoptions = @&user_ptr->gl_ttyoptions;
  user_record.va_backspace  = @&user_ptr->gl_backspace;
  user_record.va_linedel    = @&user_ptr->gl_linedel;
  if (valid_write(userindex, &user_record) <= 0)
   error("fatal:acct - bad logout validuz write.\n");   
  /* zero out the user's data area. */
  zeroptr = (byte *) &user_data_ptr[ttynum]; /* point to user's area. */  
  bound = zeroptr + sizeof(struct gl_user_datum);
  for (; zeroptr < bound; zeroptr++)  
   @zeroptr = 0;
 } /* else */
    acct_reply(tid, &logoutmsg_reply, sizeof(logoutmsg_reply));
}
do_loginmsg(tid, msg_buf)
/* Procedure do_loginmsg - verify login un and pw.  Set up user data area. */
unsigned tid;
char  msg_buf[];
{
 struct ac_loginmsg   loginmsg;
 struct ac_loginmsg_reply  loginmsg_reply;
 struct va_user_record  user_record;
 struct gl_user_datum  *user_ptr;
 byte ttynum;
 unsigned userindex, bindate[2];
    char    hashpw[12];
 copy(&loginmsg, msg_buf, sizeof(loginmsg));
 ttynum = loginmsg.ac_ttynum;
 if (ttynum == 0 || (userindex = valid_hash(loginmsg.ac_username)) == 0)
  /* bad login request */
  loginmsg_reply.ac_login_success = 0;
  /* 0 = code for improper login. */
 else  /* process login */
  if (valid_read(userindex, &user_record) == 0)
   loginmsg_reply.ac_login_success = 3; /* bad login record. */ 
     else
   if (user_record.va_closed)
    loginmsg_reply.ac_login_success = 4; /* account closed. */
   else
    if (user_record.va_creditbalance <= 0)
     loginmsg_reply.ac_login_success = 2; /* no credits left. */
    else {
     hashpassword(loginmsg.ac_userpw, hashpw);
     if (!equal(hashpw, user_record.va_hashpw))
      loginmsg_reply.ac_login_success = 0;  /* bad password. */
     else {
/* login complete */
      loginmsg_reply.ac_login_success =
       user_record.va_creditbalance <= 40 ? 5 : 1;
      get_date(bindate);
      user_ptr = &user_data_ptr[ttynum];
      strcpy_at_from_star(user_ptr->gl_username, 
       loginmsg.ac_username);
      @&user_ptr->gl_useracct =
       user_record.va_useracct;
      @&user_ptr->gl_userindex = userindex;
      @&user_ptr->gl_logintime[0] = bindate[0];
      @&user_ptr->gl_logintime[1] = bindate[1];
      @&user_ptr->gl_last_creditupdate[0] = bindate[0];
      @&user_ptr->gl_last_creditupdate[1] = (bindate[1]/60) * 60;
      @&user_ptr->gl_binarylastcr[0] = bindate[0];
      @&user_ptr->gl_binarylastcr[1] = bindate[1];
            @&user_ptr->gl_creditbalance =

     The unit of cost for using GBMS is the 'credit'.    The
computer  keeps track of how many credits you have purchased
and how many you have used.  The number of credits you spend
per hour depends on the game or application you are running,
and whether you are taking advantage of any rate discounts.
      As a rule of thumb, one credit will  generally  give
you   between  three and eight minutes of game time.  At the
end of each month, you will be mailed  a  statement  listing
exactly    how  many credits you have used, and how many you
have remain- ing in your account for that month.
