kip(3,data));
 print("Phone: %s\n",str_skip(4,data));
 print("Interests: %s\n",str_skip(5,data));
 print("\n");
 opts = user_record->va_ttyoptions;
 print("    Full-duplex......... %s\n",onoff[(opts & ECHO) != 0]);
 print("    BS/space/BS echoed.. %s\n",onoff[(opts & EDEL) != 0]);
 print("    Control-H used...... %s\n",onoff[user_record->va_backspace == 0x08]);
 print("    Mail statements..... %s\n",onoff[!user_record->va_nocharge]); 
 print("    State resident...... %s\n",onoff[user_record->va_stateres]);
 print("    Name anonymous...... %s\n",onoff[user_record->va_anonymous]);
 print("    Line delete char.... %02xH\n",user_record->va_linedel);
}
int inp_data(itemnum, user_record)
/* procedure inp_data - query for an item of data to add to the user_record. 
   returns TRUE if input good, else FALSE */
int itemnum;
struct va_user_record *user_record;
{
 BUFTYPE  buffer;
 int  i;
 extern char *str_skip();
 switch(itemnum) {
case -1: 
 print("modify parameter is not unique.\n");
 break;
case 0:
 print("No such parameter.\n");
 break;
case 1:  /* Username */
 print("Enter codename (11 chars max)? ");
 readline(buffer); 
 reset_parse();
 parse(buffer,buffer);
 buffer[11] = '\0';  /* truncate zero */
 if (valid_hash(buffer) != 0)
  print("Duplicate codename.\n");
 else {
  strcpy(user_record->va_username, buffer);
  return(TRUE);
 }
 break; 
case 2:    /* Password */
 print("Enter password (11 chars max)? ");
 readline(buffer);
 reset_parse();
 parse(buffer,buffer);
 buffer[11] = '\0';
 if (buffer[0] != '\0') {
  hashpassword(buffer,user_record->va_hashpw);
  print("PW update complete.\n");
  return(TRUE);
 }
 break;
case 3:  /* Account */
 print("Enter account number (1-255)? ");
 readline(buffer);
 i = atoi(buffer);
 if (i <= 0 || i > 255)
  print("Acct value out of range.\n");
 else {
  if (i == 255)
   print("WARNING! acct set to super-user status.\n");
  user_record->va_useracct = i;
  return(TRUE);
 }
 break;
case 4:  /*  echo flag */
 print("Echo characters (yes)? ");
 user_record->va_ttyoptions &= ~ECHO;
 user_record->va_ttyoptions |= ECHO * getflag();
 return(TRUE);
 break;
case 5:  /*  edel flag */
 print("Send BS,space,BS to delete characters (yes)? ");
 user_record->va_ttyoptions &= ~EDEL;
 user_record->va_ttyoptions |= EDEL * getflag();
 return(TRUE);
 break;
case 6:  /* Control-H */
 print("Control-H instead of DEL key to erase characters (yes)? ");
 user_record->va_backspace = getflag() ? 0x08 : 0x7F;
 return(TRUE);    
 break;           
                     
case 7:  /* credit balance */
 print("Enter credit adjustment? ");
 readline(buffer);
 i = atoi(buffer);
 if (i != 0) {
  print("%d + %d = ",user_record->va_creditbalance, i);
  user_record->va_creditbalance += i;
  print("%d cr.\n",user_record->va_creditbalance);
 }
 return(TRUE);
 break;
case 8:  /* closed */
 print("Is this account open (yes) ? ");
 user_record->va_closed = !getflag();
 return(TRUE);
 break;
case 9:  /* birthdate */
{
 int m, d;
 print("Birthdate month? (1-12)? ");
 readline(buffer);
 m = atoi(buffer);
 print("Birthdate date? (1-31)? ");
 readline(buffer);
 d = atoi(buffer);
 user_record->va_birthdate = (m << 8) | (d << 1);
 return(TRUE);
 break;
}
case 10: /* rating */
 print("Credit rating (1-10)? ");
 readline(buffer);
 i = atoi(buffer);
 if (i != 0) {
  user_record->va_rating = i;
  return(TRUE);
 }
 break;
case 11:  /* linedel char */
 print("Enter line-delete code (hex, default 1B)? ");
 readline(buffer);
 i = atoh(buffer);
 if (i != 0)
  user_record->va_linedel = i;
 return(TRUE);
 break;
case 12:  /* nocharge */
 print("Charge credits to user and mail statement (yes)? ");
 user_record->va_nocharge = !getflag();
 return(TRUE);
 break;
case 13: /* name, addr1, addr2, addr3, phone, interests */
{ char         *string, *strbase;
 display(user_record);
 strbase = &user_record->va_stringbuf; /* 192 char buffer */
 print("Full name? ");
 readline(buffer);
    buffer[79] = '\0';
 strcpy(strbase,buffer);
 print("Address line 1? ");
 readline(buffer);
 buffer[79] = '\0';
 string = str_skip(1,strbase);
 strcpy(string,buffer);
 print("Address line 2? ");
 readline(buffer);
 string = str_skip(2,strbase);
 buffer[min(79,max(0,187 - (int) (string - strbase)))] = '\0';
 strcpy(string, buffer);                    
 print("Address line 3? ");
 readline(buffer);
 string = str_skip(3,strbase);
 buffer[min(79,max(0,187 - (int) (string - strbase)))] = '\0';
 strcpy(string, buffer);
 print("Phone number? "); 
 readline(buffer);
 string = str_skip(4, strbase);
 buffer[min(79,max(0,187 - (int) (string - strbase)))] = '\0';
 strcpy(string, buffer);
 string = str_skip(5, strbase);
 i = min(79,max(0,187 - (int) (string - strbase)));
  print("Interests/Hobbies (%d chars max)? ",i);
 readline(buffer);
   buffer[i] = '\0';
 strcpy(string, buffer); 
 return(TRUE);
}
case 14:  /* resident */
  print("Does the user live inside the state (yes)? ");
  user_record->va_stateres = getflag();
  return(TRUE);
  break;
  
case 15: /* anonymous */
 print("Does the user want his real name made public (yes)? ");
 user_record->va_anonymous = !getflag();
 return(TRUE);
 break;
default:
 print("No such option, try again.");
 break;
}
 return(FALSE);
}
int getflag()
/* procedure getflag - read "yes" and "no" from input and return
   proper boolean value */
{
 BUFTYPE  buf;
 extern reset_parse();
 readline(buf);
 reset_parse();
 parse(buf,buf);
 if (buf[0] != 'n')
  return(TRUE);
 else
  return(FALSE);
}
send_credits(index, numcred)
/* Procedure send_credits - send credit increase to accounting. */
unsigned index;
int   numcred;
{
 struct ac_creditmsg  creditmsg;
 struct ac_creditmsg_reply  creditmsg_reply;
 creditmsg.ac_checkword = AC_CHECKWORD;
 creditmsg.ac_msgnum = CREDITMSG;
 creditmsg.ac_userindex = index;
 creditmsg.ac_credits = numcred;
 if (send(detach(ACCOUNTING_PORT), &creditmsg, &creditmsg_reply,
  sizeof(creditmsg)) <= 0)
   error("fatal:credit - Unable to send to Acct Admin.\n");
 switch (creditmsg_reply.ac_credit_success) {
case 1: print("Credit update complete.  New balance=%d cr\n",
   creditmsg_reply.ac_creditbalance);
        break;  
case 2: print("\007Credit balance overflow!\n");
  break;
default: /* including zero */
  print("\007Error in accounting credit update.\n");
  break;
 }
}

/* sendap - send name of application to accounting admin.
 83/11/02. A. E. Klietz.
 This library routine sends the application number (apnum)
of the current application program to the accounting administrator.
It also checks the reply to see if the user's account has expired.
*/
#include "aekdef.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include "globdef.h"
#include "appdef.h"
#include "ac_msgs.h"
extern byte  My_tty;
extern unsigned  My_tid;
extern struct gl_user_datum *user_data_ptr;
sendap(apnum)
/* Procedure sendap - send application number to accounting admin. */
int  apnum;
{

/*  jourdef.h - journal file layout.
  83/11/03. A. E. Klietz.
 This file defines the format of a record in the journal file.
(/jou/journal).
 This file depends on aekdef.h.
*/
#define JR_CHECKBYTE 0x4e
enum JR_ENTRYTYPE {LOGIN_ENTRY, LOGOUT_ENTRY, NEWAP_ENTRY,
   CRASH_ENTRY, WRITEVAL_ENTRY, CREDIT_ENTRY, MAX_ENTRY};
struct jr_journal_record {
 byte  jr_checkbyte;    /* checkbyte */
 byte  jr_entry;    /* journal entry number */
 unsigned jr_bindate[2];   /* date/time of entry. */
 unsigned jr_ttynum;       /* tty used (or userindex ) */
 int   jr_credits;    /* credit balance. */
 byte  jr_apnum;        /* applcation code number. */
 byte        jr_dummy;
};
#define JR_CHECKBYTE 0x4e

   CRASH_ENTRY, WRITEVAL_ENTRY, CREDIT_ENTRY, MAX_ENTRY};

/*  Acct_subs2 - More accounting subroutines.
  83/11/03. A. E. Klietz.
*/
#define MSGBUFLEN 512
#include "aekdef.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include "globdef.h"
#include "validef.h"
#include "ac_msgs.h"
#include "appdef.h"
#include "jourdef.h"
extern struct gl_system_datum *system_data_ptr;    /* pointer to global system data. */
extern struct gl_user_datum   *user_data_ptr;      /* pointer to global user data. */
do_newapmsg(tid, msg_buf)
/* Procedure do_newapmsg - send new applcation to journal, update tables. */
unsigned tid;
char  msg_buf[];
{
 struct ac_newapmsg   newapmsg;
 struct ac_newapmsg_reply  newapmsg_reply;
 byte ttynum;
 unsigned credit_left, userindex, bindate[2];
 copy(&newapmsg, msg_buf, sizeof(newapmsg));
 ttynum = newapmsg.ac_ttynum;
 if (ttynum == 0 || newapmsg.ac_apnum >= AP_MAXNUM || newapmsg.ac_apnum < 0)
  /* bad newap request */
  newapmsg_reply.ac_apnum_success = 0;
 else  { /* process newap */
  credit_left = credit_update(ttynum, newapmsg.ac_apnum);
  @&user_data_ptr[ttynum].gl_apnum = newapmsg.ac_apnum;
  if (credit_left <= 0)
   newapmsg_reply.ac_apnum_success = 2; /* no credit left */   
  else   
   newapmsg_reply.ac_apnum_success =    
    credit_left <= 40 ? 3 : 1;  /* 3 if low, else 1 if ok. */
  write_journal(NEWAP_ENTRY, ttynum, newapmsg.ac_apnum);
  
  }
 acct_reply(tid, &newapmsg_reply, sizeof(newapmsg_reply));
}
do_intlockmsg(tid, msg_buf)
/* Procedure do_intlockmsg - interlock accounting until signal sent. */
unsigned tid;
char  msg_buf[];
{
 struct ac_intlockmsg   intlockmsg;
 struct ac_intlockmsg_reply  intlockmsg_reply;
 copy(&intlockmsg, msg_buf, sizeof(intlockmsg));
 acct_reply(tid, &intlockmsg_reply, sizeof(intlockmsg_reply));
 await(ACCOUNTING_PORT);  /* block until port signaled */
 read_port(ACCOUNTING_PORT); /* clear any other pending signals */
}
do_readmsg(tid, msg_buf)
/* Procedure do_readmsg - read user's validation file. */
unsigned tid;
char  msg_buf[];
{
 struct ac_readmsg   readmsg;
 struct ac_readmsg_reply  readmsg_reply;
 copy(&readmsg, msg_buf, sizeof(readmsg));
 readmsg_reply.ac_readmsg_success =
  valid_read(readmsg.ac_userindex, &readmsg_reply.ac_user_record);
 acct_reply(tid, &readmsg_reply, sizeof(readmsg_reply));
}

/*  credit_update - update a user's credit status.
  83/11/03.  A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include "aekdef.h"
#include "appdef.h"
#include "globdef.h"
extern struct  gl_user_datum *user_data_ptr;
extern struct  gl_system_datum *system_data_ptr;
#asm "seg 2" /* force into constant area */
static int  rate_list[] = {   /* minutes per credit */
32767,   /* login is free */
6,       /* menu.      6 min/cr = 10 cr/hour */
3,       /* scepter.   3 min/cr = 20 cr/hour */
5,       /* talk.      5 min/cr = 12 cr/hour */
4,       /* mail.      4 min/cr = 15 cr/hour */
4,       /* diplomacy. 4 min/cr = 15 cr/hour */
32767};  /* system is free */
int credit_update(ttynum, newapnum)
/* procedure credit_update - update a user's credit balance, and
return the balance.  ttynum = tty of user.  newapnum = new application
number for carrying_over fractional credits.  <= 0 if same task.  
*/
int  ttynum, newapnum;
{
 int  credits_left;
 unsigned remainder, rate, minutes, olddate[2], bindate[2], credits_used;
 extern unsigned mindiff();
 struct gl_user_datum *user_ptr;
 
 if (newapnum < -1 || newapnum >= AP_MAXNUM)
  error("fatal:credit - bad app number.\n");
 user_ptr = &user_data_ptr[ttynum];
 olddate[0] = @&user_ptr->gl_last_creditupdate[0];
 olddate[1] = @&user_ptr->gl_last_creditupdate[1];
 get_date(bindate);
 bindate[1] = (bindate[1] / 60) * 60; /* truncate to nearest minute */
 minutes = mindiff(olddate, bindate);
 minutes = minutes + @&user_ptr->gl_minute_remainder;
 rate = rate_list[@&user_ptr->gl_apnum] + /* give nonpeak time bonus */
  (@&system_data_ptr->gl_peakflag ? 0 : 1);
  rate++;  /* give bonus rate for nonpeak time */
 credits_left = @&user_ptr->gl_creditbalance;
 credits_used = minutes / rate; /* Integer division truncates */
                     
    remainder = minutes % rate;           
 { /* tack fractional credit onto next application. */ 
        int newrate;
  
   if (newapnum <= 0) /* if same application as before */
   newrate = rate;
   else  
   newrate = rate_list[newapnum] + /* give nonpeak bonus */
    (@&system_data_ptr->gl_peakflag ? 0 : 1);
/* Change remaining minutes to new app rate, rounding off. */  
                                              
    @&user_ptr->gl_minute_remainder = (remainder * newrate + rate/2) / rate;
 }                                               
 if (credits_used > 32767 || credits_left - (int) credits_used < 0)
  credits_left = 0;
 else
  credits_left = credits_left - (int) credits_used;
 @&user_ptr->gl_creditbalance = credits_left

/*  kill_all_tasks - Systematically kill everything except caller.
  83/11/12.  A. E. Klietz.
  This nasty program sends HANGUP signals to all ttys except the
calling tty, and sends BREAK signals to all tasks connected to ports
17-24 except the calling port (usually /sys/accounting)
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include "aekdef.h"
#include "globdef.h"    /* NUMDEVICES */
#define EXC_HANGUP 1
#define EXC_BREAK 2
#define IDLE   0x0004
extern unsigned My_tid;
extern char My_tty, User_num;
kill_all_tasks()
{
 int  i, count = 0;
 int  tid;
 if (User_num != -1)
  error("fatal:kill - illegal access.\n");
 print("Hanging up all ttys...  ");
 for (i=0; i < NUMDEVICES; i++)
  if (i != My_tty && (tid = tty_tid(i)) != IDLE && tid != -1) {
   print("Hangup tty%d.\n",i);
   set_exception(tid, EXC_HANGUP, 0);  /* force hangup */
   count++;
  }
 sleep(count*10); /* wait for hangups to complete. */ 
 print("Breaking all ports... ");
 for (i=17; i <= 24; i++)
  if ((tid = detach(i)) != 0 && tid != My_tid) {
   print("Breaking port %d.\n",i);
   set_exception(tid, EXC_BREAK, 0);
  }
 print("All tasks stopped.\n");
}
rint("Hanging up all ttys...  ");
 for (i=0; i < NUMDEVICES; i++)

   set_exception(tid, EXC_HANGUP, 0);  /* force hangup */
   count++;

/*  valmsgs - request operations on validation file to accounting.
  83/11/22.  A. E. Klietz.
  interlock_validfile(flag)   TRUE to lock, FALSE to unlock.
  read_valid(userindex, &user_record) read data to user_record
    returns 0 if bad index.
  write_valid(userindex, &user_record) write user_record to file
    returns 0 if bad write.
  str_skip(count, string) skip forward *count* strings in a multiple
    string array.
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include "aekdef.h"
#include "globdef.h"
#include "validef.h"
#include "ac_msgs.h"
extern char  My_tty;
extern struct gl_user_datum *user_data_ptr;
 
interlock_validfile(flag)
/* procedure interlock_validfile - halt the accounting administrator if TRUE,
   restart the administrator if FALSE */
int  flag;
{ 
 static boolean  prevflag;
 struct ac_intlockmsg  intlockmsg;
 struct ac_intlockmsg_reply intlockmsg_reply;
 if (flag == prevflag)
  error("fatal:ilock - lock flag did not change!\n");
 if (!flag) {
  signal(ACCOUNTING_PORT); /* wake up accounting */
  prevflag = flag;
  breakable(); /* restore interrupts */
 }
 else { /* put accounting to sleep */
 unbreakable(); /* disable interrupts */
 intlockmsg.ac_checkword = AC_CHECKWORD;
 intlockmsg.ac_msgnum = INTLOCKMSG;
  
 if (send(detach(ACCOUNTING_PORT), &intlockmsg, &intlockmsg_reply,
  sizeof(intlockmsg)) <= 0)
   error("fatal:ilock - unable to send to acct admin.\n");
 prevflag = flag;
    }
}
 
read_valid(userindex, user_record)
unsigned userindex;
struct  va_user_record *user_record;
{ 
 struct ac_readmsg  readmsg;
 struct ac_readmsg_reply readmsg_reply;
 readmsg.ac_checkword = AC_CHECKWORD;
 readmsg.ac_msgnum = READMSG;
 readmsg.ac_ttynum   = My_tty;
 readmsg.ac_userindex = userindex;
 if (send(detach(ACCOUNTING_PORT), &readmsg, &readmsg_reply,
  sizeof(readmsg)) <= 0)
   error("fatal:vread - unable to send to acct admin.\n");
 
 if (readmsg_reply.ac_readmsg_success == 0)
  error("Bad validuz read.\n");
 else
  copy(user_record, &readmsg_reply.ac_user_record, 
   sizeof(struct va_user_record));
}
 
write_valid(userindex, user_record)
unsigned userindex;
struct  va_user_record *user_record;
{ 
 struct ac_writemsg  writemsg;
 struct ac_writemsg_reply writemsg_reply;
 writemsg.ac_checkword = AC_CHECKWORD;
 writemsg.ac_msgnum = WRITEMSG;
 writemsg.ac_ttynum  = My_tty;
 writemsg.ac_apnum = @&user_data_ptr[My_tty].gl_apnum;
    user_record->va_userindex = userindex;
  
 copy(&writemsg.ac_user_record, user_record,
  sizeof(struct va_user_record));
 if (send(detach(ACCOUNTING_PORT), &writemsg, &writemsg_reply,
  sizeof(writemsg)) <= 0)
   error("fatal:vwrite - unable to send to acct admin.\n");
 
 if (writemsg_reply.ac_writemsg_success == 0)
  error("Bad validuz write.\n");
}

int  count;
char *string;
{
 register   i;
 for (i=1; i<=count; i++) 
  while (*string++ != '\0')
   ;
 return(string);
}

/*  Mail - mail out billing statements to users.
   83/11/27. A. E. Klietz
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include "aekdef.h"
#include "validef.h"

#include "jourdef.h"
extern byte My_tty;
main()
{
 char buffer[80];
 struct va_user_record user_record;
 int  upper, lower, i;
 if (attach(ACCOUNTING_PORT) != My_tty)
  error("System must be shut down before mailing statements.\n");
 if (attach(16) != My_tty)
  error("Line printer is busy.\n");
    tfprintf(stdout, "Enter lower bound of userindexes? ");
 gets(buffer, BUFLEN);
 lower = atoi(buffer);
 tfprintf(stdout, "Enter upper bound of userindexes? ");
 gets(buffer, BUFLEN);
 upper = atoi(buffer);
 if (lower > upper || lower <= 0 || upper <= 0)
  error("Bad lower or upper bound.\n");
 tfprintf(stdout, "Ready printer and press return.\n");
 gets(buffer, BUFLEN);
 for (i=lower; i <= upper; i++) {
  if (valid_read(i, &user_record) <= 0)
   error("Bad valid read on userindex %04u.\n",i);
  if (user_record.va_userindex == 0)
   tfprintf(stdout, "userindex %04u empty.\n", i);
  else
   print_stmt(i, &user_record);
 }
 tfprintf(stdout, "printing finished.\n");
}
print_stmt(index, user_record)
unsigned index;
struct  va_user_record  *user_record;
{
 FILE *lp, *fp;
 char name[20];
 struct jr_journal_record journal_record;
 int  lastcr;
 unsigned bindate[2], day1, day2;
 extern char *timeconv(), *dateconv();
 day1 = count_days(user_record->va_lastlogin);
 get_date(bindate);
 day2 = count_days(bindate);
 if (user_record->va_closed || day2 - day1 > 365 || day2 - day1 > 75 &&
  user_record->va_creditbalance == 0) { /* delete account */
  tfprintf(stdout,"Erasing inactive account: %04u.\n",index);
  if (valid_delete(index) == 0)
   error("error deleting account.\n");
  return(0);
 }
 if (user_record->va_nocharge) /* dont mail statement */
  return(0);
 if ((lp = fopen("$lpt","wv")) == 0)
  error("Unable to open $lpt.\n");
 print_header(lp, index, user_record);
 tsprintf(name, "/jou/%04u", index);  
 if ((fp = fopen(name,"rv")) == 0)
  tfprintf(stdout, "%s - empty or unable to open.\n",name);   
 else {
  lastcr = -32767;
  while (fget(&journal_record, 12, fp) == 12) {
   show_journal(lp, &journal_record);
   if (journal_record.jr_entry != CREDIT_ENTRY)
    lastcr = journal_record.jr_credits;
  }    
     fclose(fp);
 
  if (lastcr != -32767 && lastcr != user_record->va_creditbalance)
   tfprintf(stdout, "\007validuz: %d cr.  and journal: %d cr. do not balance.\n",
    user_record->va_creditbalance, lastcr);
     
  tfprintf(lp,"\014");  /* form feed */
 }
 tfprintf(lp,"\n\nCurrent credit balance = %d cr.\n",
   user_record->va_creditbalance, lastcr);
 tfprintf(lp,"Last login was on: %s, %s.\n\n",dateconv(user_record->va_lastlogin),
  timeconv(user_record->va_lastlogin));
 tfprintf(lp,"Current conversion rate is 8 credits per dollar.\n");
 if (user_record->va_creditbalance < 1000) {
  tfprintf(lp,"\n\n To add credits to your account, please mail a check or money order to:\n");
  tfprintf(lp,"          GamBit Multi Systems\n"); 
  tfprintf(lp,"          P.O. Box 23495\n");
  tfprintf(lp,"          Minneapolis, MN  55423\n");
  tfprintf(lp,"\n(Be sure to write your codename somewhere on the check.)\n");
 }
 tfprintf(lp,"\n\nThank You!\n");
 tfprintf(lp,"\014"); /* form feed */
 fclose(lp);
}
print_header(lp, index, user_record)
FILE *lp;
unsigned index;
struct va_user_record *user_record;
{
 char *skip_string();
 tfprintf(lp,"\n                G a m B i t    M u l t i   S y s t e m s\n");
 tfprintf(lp,"                              Statement of Account\n\n\n");
 tfprintf(lp,"Codename: %s.  Userindex: %04u.\n",
  user_record->va_username, index);
 tfprintf(lp,"\n%s\n%s\n%s\n%s\n\n",
  user_record->va_stringbuf,
  skip_string(1, user_record->va_stringbuf),
  skip_string(2, user_record->va_stringbuf),
  skip_string(3, user_record->va_stringbuf));
}
char *skip_string(count, string)
/* procedure skip_string - skip 'count' strings forward in a list of strings. */
int  count;
char *string;
{
 register   i;
 for (i=1; i<=count; i++) 
  while (*string++ != '\0')
   ;
 return(string);
}

     Currently,  GBMS  offers  a  peak  time/non-peak   time
discount.  If  you  access the GamBit system during non-peak
hours, you get a 25 percent discount below the normal rates.
     Nonpeak hours have been set from 2 am through 12  noon.

it economical to offer rate  discounts.    These  hours  are

/*  modval - modify validation file.
  83/10/10.  A. E. Klietz.
  This program handles all modifications of the validation file
(/val/validuz) outside of normal accounting.
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include "aekdef.h"
#include <dev.h>
#include "globdef.h"
#include "validef.h"
#include "ac_msgs.h"
#define EXC_BREAK 2
extern  byte User_num;
struct gl_user_datum *user_data_ptr;
struct gl_system_datum *system_data_ptr;
static struct cmdlist_type cmdlist[] = {
 "create", 1,
 "end", 2,
 "exit", 2,
 "help", 4,
 "list", 5,
 "modify", 6,
 "receive", 7,
 "show", 8
};
#define CMDLIST_LEN (sizeof(cmdlist) / sizeof(struct cmdlist_type))
static struct cmdlist_type modlist[] = {
 "account", 3,
 "anonymous",15,
 "birthdate", 9,
 "charge", 12,
 "codename", 1,
 "control-h", 6,
 "credit", 7,
 "echo", 4,
 "edel", 5,
 "linedel", 11,
 "mailaddr", 13,
 "open", 8,
 "password", 2,
 "rating", 10,
 "state_resident",14
};
#define MODLIST_LEN (sizeof(modlist) / sizeof(struct cmdlist_type))
main()
{
BUFTYPE buffer;
int  len;
FILE *fp;
extern break_handler();
 if (User_num != -1)
  error("fatal:modval - illegal user access.\n");
 unbreakable(); /* disable breaks here. */
 set_glob();    /* set global pointers */
  
 print(" \n");
 
 exc_handler(EXC_BREAK, 0, break_handler);
    if(!setexit()) {
        len = char_waiting(stdin);
        if (len > 0) {
     for (len=char_waiting(stdin);  len >= 1; len--)
        getchar(stdin);   /* skip bad parity characters */
         print("*INPUT ERR*\n");
        }
  else
   print("*INT*\n");
 }
 breakable(); /* enable breaks. */
 for (;;) {  /* loop forever. */
  do {
   print("\n");
   print("Validation command? ");
   len = readline(buffer);
   if (len == EOF)   /* force a hangup command. */
    exit(0);
  } while (len == 0);
  docommand(buffer);
 }
}
docommand(command)
/* Procedure docommand - process command. */
char *command;
{
 BUFTYPE word;
 int  cmdnum, i;
 struct va_user_record user_record;
 extern reset_parse();
 reset_parse();
 if (parse(command,word) == 0)
  return(0);
 cmdnum = binary_wordmatch(word, cmdlist, CMDLIST_LEN);
 
 switch (cmdnum) {
 case -1: print("\"%s\" is not unique.\n",word);
    break;  /* not unique. */
    case  0: print("\"%s\" is not a command, type 'help' for help.\n",
                word);
    break;
    case  1: /* create */
  zero(&user_record, sizeof(user_record));  /* zero record */    
   user_record.va_ttyoptions = ECHO + EDIT + ETAB + ERS
    + EDEL + XON_XOFF + MAPCR;
   user_record.va_backspace = 0x08;
   user_record.va_linedel = 0x1B;
   user_record.va_stateres = TRUE;
   get_date(&user_record.va_lastlogin);
   for (i=1; i<=MODLIST_LEN; i++)
    while(!inp_data(i, &user_record))
     print("Invalid parameter, reenter.\n");
   print("Adding %s to validation file..\n",user_record.va_username);
   interlock_validfile(TRUE);  /* freeze accounting */
   if ((user_record.va_userindex = i = valid_create(&user_record.va_username)) == 32767)
    print("Duplicate entry.\n");
   else
    if (i == 0)
     print("Unable to create entry.\n");
    else 
     if (valid_write(i, &user_record) == 0)
      print("Bad record write (%d).\n",i);
     else
      print("Entry for %s created.\n",user_record.va_username);
   interlock_validfile(FALSE);  /* restore accounting */
   break;   
 case 2:  /* exit */
   print("Returning to control program..\n");  
   transform("/cmds/control", 0, 0);
   break;
 case 4:  /* help */
    dohelp(command, "/help/modval");
    break;
 case 5:  /* list */
   print("The available validation commands are:\n");
   cmd_display(cmdlist, CMDLIST_LEN);
   print("\nThe available modify parameters are:\n");
   cmd_display(modlist, MODLIST_LEN);
   break;
 case 6:  /* modify */
   parse(command, word);
   if ((i = atoi(word)) == 0 && (i = valid_hash(word)) == 0)
    print("No such username.\n");
   else {
    if (read_valid(i, &user_record) == 0) 
     print("Bad valid read.\n");
    else {
     parse(command, word);
     cmdnum = binary_wordmatch(word, modlist, MODLIST_LEN);
     if (cmdnum == 1)
      print("Close and recreate account to change name.\n");
     else
      if (inp_data(cmdnum, &user_record) == TRUE) 
       if (write_valid(i, &user_record) == 0)
        print("Bad valid write.\n");
       else
        print("Entry modified.\n");
       }
   }
   break;
    
 case 7: /* receive credits.*/
   parse(command,word);
   if ((i = valid_hash(word)) == 0)
    print("No such user.\n");
   else {
     int  credits;
    parse(command, word);
    if ((credits = atoi(word)) < 1 || credits > 200) {
     if (credits == 0) {
      print("Missing dollar parameter.\n");
      return(-1);
     }
     print("\007 Are you SURE it's %d dollars? ",credits);
     if (getflag() == FALSE)
      return(-1);
    }
     if (abs(credits) >= 4096) {
      print("\007 Credit account overflow!\n");
      return(-1);
     }
     print("Adding %d dollars to account.\n",credits);
/* the following statement gives 8 credits per dollar. */
     credits *= 8;
     send_credits(i, credits);
   }
   break;
 case 8: /* show */
   parse(command,word);
   if ((i = atoi(word)) == 0 && (i = valid_hash(word)) == 0)   
    print("No such user.\n");
            else 
    if (read_valid(i, &user_record) == 0)
     print("Bad valid read.\n");
    else
     display(&user_record);
   break;
 default:
   print("warn:modval - no such command index!\n");
    break;
 }
}
cmd_display(list, list_len)
struct cmdlist_type list[];
int  list_len;
{
 int i, skip, index;
 skip = (list_len / 3) + 1;
 for (i=0; i < skip * 3; i++) {
  if (i % 3 == 0)
   print("\n");
  index = (i % 3)*skip + i/3;
  if (index < list_len)
   print("%-11s",list[index].cmd);
 }
 print("\n");
}
display(user_record)
/* procedure display - display user's data record on output. */
struct va_user_record *user_record;
{
 char *data;
 unsigned opts;
 char onoff[2][6];
 extern char *str_skip();
 strcpy(onoff[FALSE],"*OFF*");
 strcpy(onoff[TRUE],"*ON*");
    print("\n");
 print("%s, Account: %03d.  Userindex: %04d.  Balance: %d cr.\n",
    user_record->va_username, user_record->va_useracct,
    user_record->va_userindex, user_record->va_creditbalance);
 print("Last login: %s, %s.\n",
    dateconv(user_record->va_lastlogin),
    timeconv(user_record->va_lastlogin));
 print("Bday: %s, CRat: %d.   Account: %s.\n",
    dateconv(&user_record->va_birthdate), user_record->va_rating,
    user_record->va_closed ? "CLOSED" : "Open");
 data = user_record->va_stringbuf;
 print(" Name: %s\n",str_skip(0,data));
 print("Addr1: %s\n",str_skip(1,data));
 print("Addr2: %s\n",str_skip(2,data));

/*  control - GamBit system control program.
  83/10/01.  A. E. Klietz.
  This system control program handles most system maintenance
and control functions.
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include "aekdef.h"
#include <dev.h>
#include "globdef.h"
#include "jourdef.h"
#include "appdef.h"
#define BEQUEATH_TTY 0x04
#define EXC_BREAK 2
#define IDLE   0x0004
extern unsigned My_tid, Dads_tid;
extern char My_tty, User_num;
struct gl_user_datum  *user_data_ptr;
struct gl_system_datum  *system_data_ptr;
static struct cmdlist_type cmdlist[] = {
    "account", 14,  "broadcast", 3,
 "close", 1,  "crash", 2,  "end", 4,  "exit", 4,
 "help", 12,  "idle", 15,     "list", 11, "logoff", 6,"menu", 4,
  "modval",13,  "monitor", 7,  "open", 8,  "quit", 4,
 "shutdown", 10  };
  
#define CMDLIST_LEN (sizeof(cmdlist) / sizeof(struct cmdlist_type))
main()
{
BUFTYPE buffer;
int  len;
FILE *fp;
extern break_handler();
 
 unbreakable(); /* disable breaks here. */
 set_glob();    /* set global pointers */
  
 print(" \n");
 print("Type 'help' for help.\n");
 
 exc_handler(EXC_BREAK, 0, break_handler);
    if(!setexit()) {
        len = char_waiting(stdin);
        if (len > 0) {
     for (len=char_waiting(stdin);  len >= 1; len--)
        getchar(stdin);   /* skip bad parity characters */
         print("*INPUT ERR*\n");
        }
  else
   print("*INT*\n");
 }
 breakable(); /* enable breaks. */
 for (;;) {  /* loop forever. */
  do {
   print("\n");
   print("Enter control command? ");
   len = readline(buffer);
   if (len == EOF)   /* force a hangup command. */
    exit(0);
  } while (len == 0);
  docommand(buffer);
 }
}
docommand(command)
/* Procedure docommand - process menu command. */
char *command;
{
 BUFTYPE word;
 int  cmdnum, i;
 extern reset_parse();
 reset_parse();
 if (parse(command,word) == 0)
  return(0);
 cmdnum = binary_wordmatch(word, cmdlist, CMDLIST_LEN);
 
 switch (cmdnum) {
 case -1: print("\"%s\" is not unique.\n", word);
    break; /* not unique. */
    case  0: print("\"%s\" is not a command, type 'help' for help.\n",
                word);
    break;
 case  1: /* close */
   @&system_data_ptr->gl_sysopen = FALSE;
   print("Logins disabled.  System closed.\n");
   break;
 case  2: /* crash */
   print("WARNING! Are you sure you want to do an emergency crash? ");
   readline(word);
   reset_parse();
   parse(word,word);
   if (word[0] == 'y') {
    @&system_data_ptr->gl_sysidle = TRUE;
    kill_all_tasks();
/* Kill all user tasks except ours. */
    print("System crashed.\n");    
    if (Dads_tid == IDLE)
     transform("/cmds/qnxsh", 0, 0);
    else
     exit(0);
   } 
   break;   
 case 3: /* broadcast */
 {
   extern  int  parseloc;
   int  port_num, len;
   FILE *fp;
   char port_name[8];
  
   len = parseloc;
         while (len <= 0) {
    print("Enter message to broadcast? ");
    len = readline(command);
    reset_parse();
   }
   while (isseparator(command[parseloc]))
    parseloc++;  /* skip leading spaces */
   for (port_num = 0; port_num < NUMDEVICES; port_num++) 
    if (@&user_data_ptr[port_num].gl_userindex > 0) {
     strcpy_star_from_at(port_name, user_data_ptr[port_num].gl_ttyname);
     fp = fopen(port_name, "wv");
     tfprintf(fp, "\n\007%s   Note: %s\n\n", time(),
     &command[parseloc]);
     fclose(fp);
    }
   print("Message sent.\n");
   break;
 }
 case 4: /* exit */
   print("Returning to main menu..\n");
   transform("/sys/menu", 1, 0);
   break;
  case 12: /* help */
    dohelp(command, "/help/control");
    break;
 case 11: /* list commands */
   print("The available control commands are:\n");
   {
    int i, skip, index;
    skip = (CMDLIST_LEN / 3) + 1;
    for (i=0; i < skip * 3; i++) {
     if (i % 3 == 0)
      print("\n");
     index = (i % 3)*skip + i/3;
     if (index < CMDLIST_LEN)
      print("%-11s",cmdlist[index].cmd);
    }
   }
   print("\n");
   break;
 case  6: /* logoff */
  {
  int  port_num, tid;
  if (parse(command, word) <= 0) {
   print("Missing tty number.\n");
   return(-1);
  }
  port_num = atoi(word);
  print("Are you sure you want to log off tty%d? ",port_num);
  readline(word);
  reset_parse();
  parse(word, word);
  if (word[0] == 'y') {
   while ((tid = tty_tid(port_num)) != IDLE)
    set_exception(tid, 0x0001, 0); /* force hangup */
   print("tty%d logged off.\n",port_num);
  }
  break;
 }
 case  7: /* monitor */
 {
  int  port_num;
  if (parse(command, word) <= 0) {
   print("Missing tty number.\n");
   return(-1);
  }
  if ((port_num = atoi(word)) < 0 || port_num > NUMDEVICES)
   print("Bad port number.\n");  
  else {
   int *monflag;
            monflag = &user_data_ptr[port_num].gl_monitor;
   if (@monflag != 0)
    if (@monflag == (My_tty == 0 ? -1 : My_tty)) {
     @monflag = 0;
     print("Monitoring terminated on tty%d.\n",port_num);
    }
    else
     print("Port already monitored elsewhere.\n");
   else {
    @monflag = (My_tty == 0) ? -1 : My_tty;
    /* special case.. port zero monitor = -1 in table. */
    print("Monitoring initiated on tty%d.\n", port_num);
   }
  }
  break;
 }
 case  8: /* open */
   @&system_data_ptr->gl_sysidle = FALSE;
   @&system_data_ptr->gl_sysopen = TRUE;
   print("Logins enabled.  System open.\n");
   break;
 case 10: /* shutdown */
 {
   boolean  bootflag;
   parse(command,word);
   bootflag = (word[0] != '\0') && subset(word, "boot");
   print("Are you sure you want to shut down the system? ");
   readline(word);
   reset_parse();
   parse(word, word);
   if (word[0] == 'y') {
    int  minutes, i;
    
    print("How many minutes until shutdown? ");
    readline(word);
    reset_parse();
    minutes = atoi(word);
    do {
     for (i=0; i <= NUMDEVICES; i++)
      if (@&user_data_ptr[i].gl_userindex > 0) {
       FILE *fp;
       strcpy_star_from_at(word, user_data_ptr[i].gl_ttyname);
       fp = fopen(word, "wv");
       tfprintf(fp, "\n%s. \007System shutdown in %d minutes.\n",
        time(), minutes);
       if (minutes <= 5)
        tfprintf(fp, "Logins disabled, please log out.\n");
       fclose(fp);
      }
     if (minutes <= 5)
      @&system_data_ptr->gl_sysopen = FAL
d
/*  callbrk.c - call, transform, and break routines.
  83/11/06. A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include <stdio.h>
#include <dev.h>
#include "aekdef.h"
#define BEQUEATH_TTY 0x04
extern char  My_priority;
call(cmd, arg1, arg2)
/* Procedure call - call another program and return. */
char *cmd, *arg1, *arg2;
{
 create(0, BEQUEATH_TTY, My_priority, 0, 0, cmd, arg1, arg2, 0);
}
transform(cmd, arg1, arg2)
/* Procedure transform - change into another program and do not return. */
char *cmd, *arg1, *arg2;
{
 create(1, BEQUEATH_TTY, My_priority, 0, 0, cmd, arg1, arg2, 0);
}
break_handler()
/* Procedure break_handler - process break key. */
{
 extern int Exc_bits, Exc_pending;
 struct dev_entry buffer; 
 char ch, devname[8];
  get_attr(stdout, &buffer);
/* Add leading dollar sign to make legal device name. */
 
  devname[0] = '$';
  strcpy(&devname[1],buffer.dev_name);
  fclose(stdout);
  if ((stdout = fopen(devname, "wv")) == 0)
   error("break:fatal - cant reopen output.\n");
        fclose(stdin);
        if ((stdin = fopen(devname, "rv")) == 0)
           error("break:fatal - cant reopen input.\n");
 reset();
}

list_cmds()
/* list available commands */
{
  int  i, skip, index;
  print("The available menu commands are:\n");
  {
   skip = (CMDLIST_LEN / 3) + 1;
   for (i=0; i < skip * 3; i++) {
    if (i % 3 == 0)
     print("\n");
    index = (i % 3)*skip + i/3;
    if (index < CMDLIST_LEN)
     print("%-11s",cmdlist[index].cmd);
   }
  }
  print("\n");
}
user_display(index)
/* procedure user_display - display some information about user */
unsigned index;
{
 struct va_user_record user_record;
 BUFTYPE word;
 extern char *str_skip();
 int  i;
 if (read_valid(index, &user_record) != 0) {
  user_record.va_username[0] = toupper(user_record.va_username[0]);
  user_record.va_stringbuf[0] = toupper(user_record.va_stringbuf[0]);
  
  print("%s's real name is %s.  %s's interests are\n",
   user_record.va_username, (user_record.va_anonymous ?
   "Anonymous" : user_record.va_stringbuf),
   user_record.va_username);
  print("%s\n", str_skip(5, user_record.va_stringbuf));
  reset_parse();
  strcpy(word, str_skip(3, user_record.va_stringbuf));
  if (word[0] == '\0') {
   reset_parse();
   strcpy(word, str_skip(2, user_record.va_stringbuf));
  }
   for (i=0; word[i] != '\0'; i++)   
    if (atoi(&word[i]) != 0) /* dont copy zip code */
     word[i--] = '\0';
   print("%s lives in %s.\n", user_record.va_username, 
    word);
 }
}

     December 23rd, 1983
Sirs:
      In my letter  dated  November  23rd,  I  reported  a
serious  bug  in  the  QNX  file administrator (ver 1.1, Ser
1442)  that  halted  further   development   work   on   QNX
applications by  our  company.  In this letter, I noted that
scheduled plans for beta-testing  of  our  QNX  applications
would  be  cancelled  until this bug could be corrected, and
also that our interest in 10-20 additional licenses would be
discouraged while this bug existed.
      To date, this bug has not been corrected.   We  have
regularly  and  diligently  searched your Update service for
new bug corrections, but found no new corrections since  the
release of QNX 1.1 on August 21st, 1983.
      We are interested in knowing if Quantum is currently
supporting   its   software,   and  if  corrections will  be
forthcoming in the forseeable future.  If not, we would like
to be informed so we may drop  QNX  in    favor  of  a  more
supported operating system.
